## 44. `src/main/java/org/example/sto/repository/OrderRepository.java`

Строк: 361

``java
package org.example.sto.repository;

import org.example.sto.db.ConnectionManager;
import org.example.sto.model.ClientOrder;
import org.example.sto.model.Order;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderStatus;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderStatusLogEntry;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderServiceItem;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderSummary;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.sql.CallableStatement;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.sql.Types;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;

/**
 * Доступ к заявкам. Операции изменения состояния выполняются через хранимые
 * функции PostgreSQL (create_order, add_service_to_order, close_order),
 * что гарантирует соблюдение бизнес-правил на стороне БД.
 */
public final class OrderRepository {

    private final ConnectionManager connectionManager;

    public OrderRepository(ConnectionManager connectionManager) {
        this.connectionManager = connectionManager;
    }

    public List<OrderSummary> findAllSummaries() {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("orders.findAllSummaries", connection -> {
            String sql = """
                    SELECT order_id, client, car, status, total_cost
                    FROM v_order_total
                    ORDER BY order_id DESC
                    """;
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
                 ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                List<OrderSummary> result = new ArrayList<>();
                while (resultSet.next()) {
                    result.add(new OrderSummary(
                            resultSet.getInt("order_id"),
                            resultSet.getString("client"),
                            resultSet.getString("car"),
                            resultSet.getString("status"),
                            resultSet.getBigDecimal("total_cost")
                    ));
                }
                return result;
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Заявки только указанного клиента — используется ролью client.
     */
    public List<OrderSummary> findSummariesByClientId(int clientId) {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("orders.findSummariesByClientId", connection -> {
            String sql = """
                    SELECT v.order_id, v.client, v.car, v.status, v.total_cost
                    FROM v_order_total v
                    JOIN orders o ON o.id = v.order_id
                    WHERE o.client_id = ?
                    ORDER BY v.order_id DESC
                    """;
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
                statement.setInt(1, clientId);
                try (ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                    List<OrderSummary> result = new ArrayList<>();
                    while (resultSet.next()) {
                        result.add(new OrderSummary(
                                resultSet.getInt("order_id"),
                                resultSet.getString("client"),
                                resultSet.getString("car"),
                                resultSet.getString("status"),
                                resultSet.getBigDecimal("total_cost")
                        ));
                    }
                    return result;
                }
            }
        });
    }

    public Optional<Order> findById(int orderId) {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("orders.findById", connection -> {
            String sql = """
                    SELECT o.id, o.client_id, o.car_id, o.master_id, o.status_id,
                           s.name AS status_name, o.description, o.created_at, o.closed_at
                    FROM orders o
                    JOIN order_statuses s ON s.id = o.status_id
                    WHERE o.id = ?
                    """;
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
                statement.setInt(1, orderId);
                try (ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                    return resultSet.next() ? Optional.of(mapOrder(resultSet)) : Optional.empty();
                }
            }
        });
    }

    public List<Order> findByClientId(int clientId) {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("orders.findByClientId", connection -> {
            String sql = """
                    SELECT o.id, o.client_id, o.car_id, o.master_id, o.status_id,
                           s.name AS status_name, o.description, o.created_at, o.closed_at
                    FROM orders o
                    JOIN order_statuses s ON s.id = o.status_id
                    WHERE o.client_id = ?
                    ORDER BY o.created_at DESC
                    """;
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
                statement.setInt(1, clientId);
                try (ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                    List<Order> orders = new ArrayList<>();
                    while (resultSet.next()) {
                        orders.add(mapOrder(resultSet));
                    }
                    return orders;
                }
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Оказанные услуги заявки с зафиксированной ценой и суммой по строке.
     */
    public List<OrderServiceItem> findServices(int orderId) {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("orders.findServices", connection -> {
            String sql = """
                    SELECT osv.id, osv.service_id, s.name, osv.quantity, osv.price_at,
                           osv.price_at * osv.quantity AS line_total
                    FROM order_services osv
                    JOIN services s ON s.id = osv.service_id
                    WHERE osv.order_id = ?
                    ORDER BY osv.id
                    """;
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
                statement.setInt(1, orderId);
                try (ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                    List<OrderServiceItem> result = new ArrayList<>();
                    while (resultSet.next()) {
                        result.add(new OrderServiceItem(
                                resultSet.getInt("id"),
                                resultSet.getInt("service_id"),
                                resultSet.getString("name"),
                                resultSet.getInt("quantity"),
                                resultSet.getBigDecimal("price_at"),
                                resultSet.getBigDecimal("line_total")
                        ));
                    }
                    return result;
                }
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Вызывает хранимую функцию create_order и возвращает id новой заявки.
     */
    public int createOrder(int clientId, int carId, Integer masterId, String description) {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("orders.createOrder", connection -> {
            String sql = "{? = call create_order(?, ?, ?, ?)}";
            try (CallableStatement statement = connection.prepareCall(sql)) {
                statement.registerOutParameter(1, Types.INTEGER);
                statement.setInt(2, clientId);
                statement.setInt(3, carId);
                if (masterId == null) {
                    statement.setNull(4, Types.INTEGER);
                } else {
                    statement.setInt(4, masterId);
                }
                statement.setString(5, description);
                statement.execute();
                return statement.getInt(1);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Вызывает add_service_to_order. Цена фиксируется на момент добавления (price_at).
     */
    public void addService(int orderId, int serviceId, int quantity) {
        connectionManager.withConnection("orders.addService", connection -> {
            String sql = "{call add_service_to_order(?, ?, ?)}";
            try (CallableStatement statement = connection.prepareCall(sql)) {
                statement.setInt(1, orderId);
                statement.setInt(2, serviceId);
                statement.setInt(3, quantity);
                statement.execute();
                return null;
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Вызывает close_order. Триггеры БД заполнят closed_at и запишут журнал статусов.
     */
    public void closeOrder(int orderId) {
        connectionManager.withConnection("orders.closeOrder", connection -> {
            String sql = "{call close_order(?)}";
            try (CallableStatement statement = connection.prepareCall(sql)) {
                statement.setInt(1, orderId);
                statement.execute();
                return null;
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Вызывает calculate_order_total.
     */
    public BigDecimal calculateTotal(int orderId) {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("orders.calculateTotal", connection -> {
            String sql = "{? = call calculate_order_total(?)}";
            try (CallableStatement statement = connection.prepareCall(sql)) {
                statement.registerOutParameter(1, Types.NUMERIC);
                statement.setInt(2, orderId);
                statement.execute();
                return statement.getBigDecimal(1);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Вызывает get_client_orders (RETURNS TABLE).
     */
    public List<ClientOrder> findClientOrders(int clientId) {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("orders.findClientOrders", connection -> {
            String sql = "SELECT order_id, car_plate, status, total_cost, created_at FROM get_client_orders(?)";
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
                statement.setInt(1, clientId);
                try (ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                    List<ClientOrder> result = new ArrayList<>();
                    while (resultSet.next()) {
                        result.add(new ClientOrder(
                                resultSet.getInt("order_id"),
                                resultSet.getString("car_plate"),
                                resultSet.getString("status"),
                                resultSet.getBigDecimal("total_cost"),
                                resultSet.getTimestamp("created_at").toLocalDateTime()
                        ));
                    }
                    return result;
                }
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Прямое изменение статуса. Нужно для демонстрации работы триггеров
     * trigger_set_closed_at и trigger_log_status_change.
     */
    public boolean updateStatus(int orderId, int statusId) {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("orders.updateStatus", connection -> {
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(
                    "UPDATE orders SET status_id = ? WHERE id = ?")) {
                statement.setInt(1, statusId);
                statement.setInt(2, orderId);
                return statement.executeUpdate() == 1;
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Количество активных заявок по мастерам — для панели загрузки.
     */
    public Map<Integer, Integer> activeOrdersByMaster() {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("orders.activeByMaster", connection -> {
            String sql = """
                    SELECT o.master_id, COUNT(*) AS cnt
                    FROM orders o
                    JOIN order_statuses s ON s.id = o.status_id
                    WHERE o.master_id IS NOT NULL
                      AND s.name IN ('новая', 'в работе')
                    GROUP BY o.master_id
                    ORDER BY o.master_id
                    """;
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
                 ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                Map<Integer, Integer> result = new HashMap<>();
                while (resultSet.next()) {
                    result.put(resultSet.getInt("master_id"), resultSet.getInt("cnt"));
                }
                return result;
            }
        });
    }

    public List<OrderStatus> findStatuses() {        return connectionManager.withConnection("orders.findStatuses", connection -> {
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(
                    "SELECT id, name FROM order_statuses ORDER BY id");
                 ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                List<OrderStatus> statuses = new ArrayList<>();
                while (resultSet.next()) {
                    statuses.add(new OrderStatus(resultSet.getInt("id"), resultSet.getString("name")));
                }
                return statuses;
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Журнал изменений статусов. Если orderId == null, возвращается весь журнал.
     */
    public List<OrderStatusLogEntry> findStatusLog(Integer orderId) {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("orders.findStatusLog", connection -> {
            String sql = """
                    SELECT id, order_id, old_status, new_status, changed_at, changed_by
                    FROM order_status_log
                    """ + (orderId == null ? "" : "WHERE order_id = ?") + " ORDER BY id DESC";
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
                if (orderId != null) {
                    statement.setInt(1, orderId);
                }
                try (ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                    List<OrderStatusLogEntry> result = new ArrayList<>();
                    while (resultSet.next()) {
                        int oldStatus = resultSet.getInt("old_status");
                        Integer oldStatusId = resultSet.wasNull() ? null : oldStatus;
                        int newStatus = resultSet.getInt("new_status");
                        Integer newStatusId = resultSet.wasNull() ? null : newStatus;
                        result.add(new OrderStatusLogEntry(
                                resultSet.getLong("id"),
                                resultSet.getInt("order_id"),
                                oldStatusId,
                                newStatusId,
                                resultSet.getTimestamp("changed_at").toLocalDateTime(),
                                resultSet.getString("changed_by")
                        ));
                    }
                    return result;
                }
            }
        });
    }

    private Order mapOrder(ResultSet resultSet) throws SQLException {
        int masterId = resultSet.getInt("master_id");
        Integer nullableMasterId = resultSet.wasNull() ? null : masterId;
        java.sql.Timestamp closedAt = resultSet.getTimestamp("closed_at");
        return new Order(
                resultSet.getInt("id"),
                resultSet.getInt("client_id"),
                resultSet.getInt("car_id"),
                nullableMasterId,
                resultSet.getInt("status_id"),
                resultSet.getString("status_name"),
                resultSet.getString("description"),
                resultSet.getTimestamp("created_at").toLocalDateTime(),
                closedAt == null ? null : closedAt.toLocalDateTime()
        );
    }
}
``

``java
package org.example.sto.repository;

import org.example.sto.db.ConnectionManager;
import org.example.sto.model.Service;

import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.sql.Types;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

public final class ServiceRepository {

    private final ConnectionManager connectionManager;

    public ServiceRepository(ConnectionManager connectionManager) {
        this.connectionManager = connectionManager;
    }

    public List<Service> findAll() {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("services.findAll", connection -> {
            String sql = "SELECT id, name, price, duration_min FROM services ORDER BY id";
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
                 ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                List<Service> services = new ArrayList<>();
                while (resultSet.next()) {
                    services.add(mapService(resultSet));
                }
                return services;
            }
        });
    }

    public Optional<Service> findById(int id) {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("services.findById", connection -> {
            String sql = "SELECT id, name, price, duration_min FROM services WHERE id = ?";
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
                statement.setInt(1, id);
                try (ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                    return resultSet.next() ? Optional.of(mapService(resultSet)) : Optional.empty();
                }
            }
        });
    }

    public Service create(String name, java.math.BigDecimal price, Integer durationMin) {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("services.create", connection -> {
            String sql = """
                    INSERT INTO services (name, price, duration_min)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?)
                    RETURNING id, name, price, duration_min
                    """;
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
                statement.setString(1, name);
                statement.setBigDecimal(2, price);
                if (durationMin == null) {
                    statement.setNull(3, Types.INTEGER);
                } else {
                    statement.setInt(3, durationMin);
                }
                try (ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                    resultSet.next();
                    return mapService(resultSet);
                }
            }
        });
    }

    private Service mapService(ResultSet resultSet) throws SQLException {
        int duration = resultSet.getInt("duration_min");
        Integer nullableDuration = resultSet.wasNull() ? null : duration;
        return new Service(
                resultSet.getInt("id"),
                resultSet.getString("name"),
                resultSet.getBigDecimal("price"),
                nullableDuration
        );
    }
}
``

``java
package org.example.sto.repository;

import org.example.sto.db.ConnectionManager;
import org.example.sto.model.User;

import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

/**
 * Доступ к таблицам roles/users. Проверка пароля выполняется в PostgreSQL
 * функцией crypt() из расширения pgcrypto, поэтому хеш не загружается в Java.
 */
public final class UserRepository {

    private final ConnectionManager connectionManager;

    public UserRepository(ConnectionManager connectionManager) {
        this.connectionManager = connectionManager;
    }

    public Optional<User> authenticate(String username, char[] password) {
        String passwordText = new String(password);
        try {
            String finalPassword = passwordText;
            return connectionManager.withConnection("users.authenticate", connection -> {
                String sql = """
                        SELECT u.id, u.username, u.full_name, r.name AS role, u.client_id
                        FROM users u
                        JOIN roles r ON r.id = u.role_id
                        WHERE u.username = ?
                          AND u.password_hash = crypt(?, u.password_hash)
                        """;
                try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
                    statement.setString(1, username);
                    statement.setString(2, finalPassword);
                    try (ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                        return resultSet.next() ? Optional.of(mapUser(resultSet)) : Optional.empty();
                    }
                }
            });
        } finally {
            java.util.Arrays.fill(password, '\0');
        }
    }

    public List<User> findAll() {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("users.findAll", connection -> {
            String sql = """
                    SELECT u.id, u.username, u.full_name, r.name AS role, u.client_id
                    FROM users u
                    JOIN roles r ON r.id = u.role_id
                    ORDER BY u.id
                    """;
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
                 ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                List<User> users = new ArrayList<>();
                while (resultSet.next()) {
                    users.add(mapUser(resultSet));
                }
                return users;
            }
        });
    }

    private User mapUser(ResultSet resultSet) throws java.sql.SQLException {
        int clientId = resultSet.getInt("client_id");
        Integer nullableClientId = resultSet.wasNull() ? null : clientId;
        return new User(
                resultSet.getInt("id"),
                resultSet.getString("username"),
                resultSet.getString("full_name"),
                resultSet.getString("role"),
                nullableClientId
        );
    }
}
``

package org.example.sto.service;

import org.example.sto.exception.AuthenticationException;
import org.example.sto.exception.ValidationException;
import org.example.sto.model.User;
import org.example.sto.repository.UserRepository;

import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Optional;

/**
 * Аутентификация пользователей. Пароль проверяется в БД через pgcrypto/bcrypt.
 */
public final class AuthService {

    private final UserRepository userRepository;

    public AuthService(UserRepository userRepository) {
        this.userRepository = userRepository;
    }

    public User login(String username, char[] password) {
        if (username == null || username.isBlank()) {
            throw new ValidationException("Логин не может быть пустым");
        }
        if (password == null || password.length == 0) {
            throw new ValidationException("Пароль не может быть пустым");
        }

        Optional<User> user = userRepository.authenticate(
                username.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT), password);
        return user.orElseThrow(() -> new AuthenticationException("Неверный логин или пароль"));
    }
}
``

package org.example.sto.service;

import org.example.sto.exception.DuplicateKeyException;
import org.example.sto.exception.EntityNotFoundException;
import org.example.sto.exception.ValidationException;
import org.example.sto.model.Car;
import org.example.sto.model.Client;
import org.example.sto.model.Master;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderStatus;
import org.example.sto.model.Service;
import org.example.sto.repository.CarRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.ClientRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.MasterRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.OrderRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.ServiceRepository;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Бизнес-операции со справочниками: клиенты, автомобили, услуги, мастера.
 * Здесь выполняется валидация входных данных до обращения к БД.
 */
public final class CatalogService {

    private final ClientRepository clientRepository;
    private final CarRepository carRepository;
    private final ServiceRepository serviceRepository;
    private final MasterRepository masterRepository;
    private final OrderRepository orderRepository;

    public CatalogService(ClientRepository clientRepository,
                          CarRepository carRepository,
                          ServiceRepository serviceRepository,
                          MasterRepository masterRepository,
                          OrderRepository orderRepository) {
        this.clientRepository = clientRepository;
        this.carRepository = carRepository;
        this.serviceRepository = serviceRepository;
        this.masterRepository = masterRepository;
        this.orderRepository = orderRepository;
    }

    public List<Client> clients() {
        return clientRepository.findAll();
    }

    public List<Car> cars() {
        return carRepository.findAll();
    }

    public List<Car> clientCars(int clientId) {
        return carRepository.findByClientId(clientId);
    }

    public List<Service> services() {
        return serviceRepository.findAll();
    }

    public List<Master> masters() {
        return masterRepository.findAll();
    }

    public List<OrderStatus> orderStatuses() {
        return orderRepository.findStatuses();
    }

    public Client clientById(int id) {
        return clientRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Клиент с id=" + id + " не найден"));
    }

    public Car carById(int id) {
        return carRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Автомобиль с id=" + id + " не найден"));
    }

    public Service serviceById(int id) {
        return serviceRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Услуга с id=" + id + " не найдена"));
    }

    public Client createClient(String fullName, String phone, String email) {
        requireText(fullName, "ФИО клиента", 150);
        requireText(phone, "Телефон", 20);
        if (!phone.matches("\\+?[0-9()\\-\\s]{5,20}")) {
            throw new ValidationException("Телефон содержит недопустимые символы");
        }
        if (email != null && !email.isBlank()) {
            requireText(email, "E-mail", 100);
            if (!email.contains("@")) {
                throw new ValidationException("E-mail должен содержать символ @");
            }
        }
        try {
            return clientRepository.create(fullName.trim(), phone.trim(), normalize(email));
        } catch (DuplicateKeyException e) {
            throw new ValidationException("Клиент с телефоном " + phone + " уже существует", e);
        }
    }

    public Car createCar(int clientId, String brand, String model, Integer year, String plateNum, String vin) {
        clientById(clientId);
        requireText(brand, "Марка", 50);
        requireText(model, "Модель", 50);
        requireText(plateNum, "Гос. номер", 15);
        if (year != null && (year < 1950 || year > 2100)) {
            throw new ValidationException("Год выпуска должен быть в диапазоне 1950..2100");
        }
        if (vin != null && !vin.isBlank() && vin.trim().length() > 17) {
            throw new ValidationException("VIN не может быть длиннее 17 символов");
        }
        try {
            return carRepository.create(clientId, brand.trim(), model.trim(), year, plateNum.trim(), normalize(vin));
        } catch (DuplicateKeyException e) {
            throw new ValidationException("Автомобиль с номером " + plateNum + " уже существует", e);
        }
    }

    public Service createService(String name, BigDecimal price, Integer durationMin) {
        requireText(name, "Наименование услуги", 150);
        if (price == null || price.signum() < 0) {
            throw new ValidationException("Стоимость услуги не может быть отрицательной");
        }
        if (durationMin != null && durationMin <= 0) {
            throw new ValidationException("Длительность услуги должна быть больше нуля");
        }
        return serviceRepository.create(name.trim(), price, durationMin);
    }

    public Client findClientByPhone(String phone) {
        return clientRepository.findByPhone(phone)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Клиент с телефоном " + phone + " не найден"));
    }

    public Car findCarByPlate(String plate) {
        return carRepository.findByPlate(plate)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Автомобиль с номером " + plate + " не найден"));
    }

    private static void requireText(String value, String field, int maxLength) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new ValidationException("Поле «" + field + "» обязательно");
        }
        if (value.trim().length() > maxLength) {
            throw new ValidationException("Поле «" + field + "» длиннее " + maxLength + " символов");
        }
    }

    private static String normalize(String value) {
        return value == null || value.isBlank() ? null : value.trim();
    }
}
``

## 49. `src/main/java/org/example/sto/service/OrderService.java`

Строк: 232

``java
package org.example.sto.service;

import org.example.sto.exception.AccessDeniedException;
import org.example.sto.exception.EntityNotFoundException;
import org.example.sto.exception.ValidationException;
import org.example.sto.model.ClientOrder;
import org.example.sto.model.Master;
import org.example.sto.model.Order;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderServiceItem;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderStatus;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderStatusLogEntry;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderSummary;
import org.example.sto.model.Permission;
import org.example.sto.model.RolePermissions;
import org.example.sto.model.Service;
import org.example.sto.model.User;
import org.example.sto.repository.CarRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.ClientRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.MasterRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.OrderRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.ServiceRepository;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * Бизнес-логика работы с заявками.
 *
 * <p>Часть правил продублирована в БД (триггеры и хранимые функции), чтобы
 * их нельзя было обойти в обход приложения. Приложение проверяет данные
 * заранее, чтобы выдавать понятные сообщения пользователю.
 */
public final class OrderService {

    private static final Set<String> CLOSED_STATUSES = Set.of("завершена", "отменена");

    private final OrderRepository orderRepository;
    private final ClientRepository clientRepository;
    private final CarRepository carRepository;
    private final MasterRepository masterRepository;
    private final ServiceRepository serviceRepository;

    public OrderService(OrderRepository orderRepository,
                        ClientRepository clientRepository,
                        CarRepository carRepository,
                        MasterRepository masterRepository,
                        ServiceRepository serviceRepository) {
        this.orderRepository = orderRepository;
        this.clientRepository = clientRepository;
        this.carRepository = carRepository;
        this.masterRepository = masterRepository;
        this.serviceRepository = serviceRepository;
    }

    public List<OrderSummary> listAll() {
        return orderRepository.findAllSummaries();
    }

    /**
     * Заявки, видимые текущему пользователю: сотрудники видят все,
     * клиент — только свои.
     */
    public List<OrderSummary> listVisible(User user) {
        if (RolePermissions.of(user.role()).can(Permission.VIEW_ALL_ORDERS)) {
            return listAll();
        }
        if (user.clientId() == null) {
            throw new AccessDeniedException(
                    "Учётная запись не привязана к карточке клиента — заявки недоступны");
        }
        return orderRepository.findSummariesByClientId(user.clientId());
    }

    public Order getOrder(int orderId) {
        return orderRepository.findById(orderId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Заявка с id=" + orderId + " не найдена"));
    }

    /**
     * Возвращает заявку, только если она доступна пользователю.
     * Клиент не может открыть заявку другого клиента по номеру.
     */
    public Order getOrderVisible(User user, int orderId) {
        Order order = getOrder(orderId);
        if (!RolePermissions.of(user.role()).can(Permission.VIEW_ALL_ORDERS)
                && (user.clientId() == null || order.clientId() != user.clientId())) {
            throw new AccessDeniedException(
                    "Заявка №" + orderId + " недоступна: она принадлежит другому клиенту");
        }
        return order;
    }

    /** Создание заявки с проверкой права CREATE_ORDER. */
    public int createOrder(User user, int clientId, int carId, Integer masterId, String description) {
        require(user, Permission.CREATE_ORDER);
        return createOrder(clientId, carId, masterId, description);
    }

    public int createOrder(int clientId, int carId, Integer masterId, String description) {
        if (clientRepository.findById(clientId).isEmpty()) {
            throw new EntityNotFoundException("Клиент с id=" + clientId + " не найден");
        }
        var car = carRepository.findById(carId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Автомобиль с id=" + carId + " не найден"));
        if (car.clientId() != clientId) {
            throw new ValidationException(
                    "Автомобиль " + car.plateNum() + " принадлежит другому клиенту (id=" + car.clientId() + ")");
        }
        if (description == null || description.isBlank()) {
            throw new ValidationException("Описание неисправности обязательно");
        }
        if (description.trim().length() > 2000) {
            throw new ValidationException("Описание не должно превышать 2000 символов");
        }
        if (masterId != null) {
            Master master = masterRepository.findById(masterId)
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Мастер с id=" + masterId + " не найден"));
            if (!master.active()) {
                throw new ValidationException("Мастер " + master.fullName() + " неактивен");
            }
        }
        return orderRepository.createOrder(clientId, carId, masterId, description.trim());
    }

    /** Добавление услуги с проверкой права ADD_ORDER_SERVICE. */
    public void addService(User user, int orderId, int serviceId, int quantity) {
        require(user, Permission.ADD_ORDER_SERVICE);
        getOrderVisible(user, orderId);
        addService(orderId, serviceId, quantity);
    }

    public void addService(int orderId, int serviceId, int quantity) {
        if (quantity <= 0 || quantity > 100) {
            throw new ValidationException("Количество должно быть от 1 до 100");
        }
        Order order = getOrder(orderId);
        if (CLOSED_STATUSES.contains(order.statusName())) {
            throw new ValidationException(
                    "Нельзя добавить услугу в заявку со статусом «" + order.statusName() + "»");
        }
        Service service = serviceRepository.findById(serviceId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Услуга с id=" + serviceId + " не найдена"));
        orderRepository.addService(orderId, service.id(), quantity);
    }

    /** Закрытие заявки с проверкой права CLOSE_ORDER. */
    public void closeOrder(User user, int orderId) {
        require(user, Permission.CLOSE_ORDER);
        getOrderVisible(user, orderId);
        closeOrder(orderId);
    }

    public void closeOrder(int orderId) {
        Order order = getOrder(orderId);
        if ("завершена".equals(order.statusName())) {
            throw new ValidationException("Заявка уже завершена");
        }
        if ("отменена".equals(order.statusName())) {
            throw new ValidationException("Нельзя завершить отменённую заявку");
        }
        orderRepository.closeOrder(orderId);
    }

    public BigDecimal calculateTotal(int orderId) {
        getOrder(orderId);
        return orderRepository.calculateTotal(orderId);
    }

    public BigDecimal calculateTotal(User user, int orderId) {
        getOrderVisible(user, orderId);
        return orderRepository.calculateTotal(orderId);
    }

    public List<OrderServiceItem> orderServices(int orderId) {
        getOrder(orderId);
        return orderRepository.findServices(orderId);
    }

    public List<OrderServiceItem> orderServices(User user, int orderId) {
        getOrderVisible(user, orderId);
        return orderRepository.findServices(orderId);
    }

    public List<ClientOrder> clientOrders(int clientId) {
        if (clientRepository.findById(clientId).isEmpty()) {
            throw new EntityNotFoundException("Клиент с id=" + clientId + " не найден");
        }
        return orderRepository.findClientOrders(clientId);
    }

    public List<OrderStatus> statuses() {
        return orderRepository.findStatuses();
    }

    public void changeStatus(int orderId, int statusId) {
        getOrder(orderId);
        boolean exists = statuses().stream().anyMatch(status -> status.id() == statusId);
        if (!exists) {
            throw new EntityNotFoundException("Статус с id=" + statusId + " не найден");
        }
        orderRepository.updateStatus(orderId, statusId);
    }

    public List<OrderStatusLogEntry> statusLog(Integer orderId) {
        return orderRepository.findStatusLog(orderId);
    }

    /**
     * Журнал статусов с учётом прав: клиент может смотреть историю только
     * собственных заявок, а весь журнал доступен лишь сотрудникам.
     */
    public List<OrderStatusLogEntry> statusLog(User user, Integer orderId) {
        if (orderId != null) {
            getOrderVisible(user, orderId);
        } else if (!RolePermissions.of(user.role()).can(Permission.VIEW_ALL_ORDERS)) {
            throw new AccessDeniedException("Журнал доступен только сотрудникам сервиса");
        }
        return orderRepository.findStatusLog(orderId);
    }

    private static void require(User user, Permission permission) {
        if (user == null || !RolePermissions.of(user.role()).can(permission)) {
            throw new AccessDeniedException("Недостаточно прав для этой операции");
        }
    }

    public Map<Integer, Integer> masterWorkload() {
        return orderRepository.activeOrdersByMaster();
    }
}
``

``java
package org.example.sto.ui;

import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import org.example.sto.config.DbSettings;
import org.example.sto.db.ConnectionManager;
import org.example.sto.model.Permission;
import org.example.sto.model.RolePermissions;
import org.example.sto.model.User;
import org.example.sto.repository.CarRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.ClientRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.MasterRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.OrderRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.ServiceRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.UserRepository;
import org.example.sto.service.AuthService;
import org.example.sto.service.CatalogService;
import org.example.sto.service.OrderService;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.ToastHost;

import java.util.concurrent.Callable;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.ThreadFactory;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

/**
 * Общие зависимости JavaFX-приложения: сервисы, фоновый исполнитель,
 * текущий пользователь и уведомления.
 */
public final class AppContext {

    private final ConnectionManager connectionManager;
    private final AuthService authService;
    private final CatalogService catalogService;
    private final OrderService orderService;
    private final ExecutorService worker;
    private ToastHost toastHost;

    private Stage stage;
    private User user;
    private Runnable logoutHandler;

    private AppContext(ConnectionManager connectionManager,
                       AuthService authService,
                       CatalogService catalogService,
                       OrderService orderService) {
        this.connectionManager = connectionManager;
        this.authService = authService;
        this.catalogService = catalogService;
        this.orderService = orderService;
        ThreadFactory factory = runnable -> {
            Thread thread = new Thread(runnable, "sto-ui-worker");
            thread.setDaemon(true);
            return thread;
        };
        this.worker = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(3, factory);
    }

    public static AppContext create(ConnectionManager connectionManager) {
        UserRepository userRepository = new UserRepository(connectionManager);
        ClientRepository clientRepository = new ClientRepository(connectionManager);
        CarRepository carRepository = new CarRepository(connectionManager);
        MasterRepository masterRepository = new MasterRepository(connectionManager);
        ServiceRepository serviceRepository = new ServiceRepository(connectionManager);
        OrderRepository orderRepository = new OrderRepository(connectionManager);

        AuthService authService = new AuthService(userRepository);
        CatalogService catalogService = new CatalogService(
                clientRepository, carRepository, serviceRepository, masterRepository, orderRepository);
        OrderService orderService = new OrderService(
                orderRepository, clientRepository, carRepository, masterRepository, serviceRepository);
        return new AppContext(connectionManager, authService, catalogService, orderService);
    }

    public static AppContext create(ConnectionManager connectionManager,
                                    AuthService authService,
                                    CatalogService catalogService,
                                    OrderService orderService) {
        return new AppContext(connectionManager, authService, catalogService, orderService);
    }

    public AuthService auth() {
        return authService;
    }

    public CatalogService catalog() {
        return catalogService;
    }

    public OrderService orders() {
        return orderService;
    }

    public DbSettings dbSettings() {
        return connectionManager.settings();
    }

    public String ping() {
        return connectionManager.ping();
    }

    public ToastHost toast() {
        if (toastHost == null) {
            toastHost = new ToastHost();
        }
        return toastHost;
    }

    public void clearToast() {
        toastHost = null;
    }

    public Stage stage() {
        return stage;
    }

    /** Корневой StackPane окна — используется для модальных окон и тостов. */
    public javafx.scene.layout.StackPane overlay() {
        if (stage == null || stage.getScene() == null
                || !(stage.getScene().getRoot() instanceof javafx.scene.layout.StackPane root)) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Окно приложения ещё не готово");
        }
        return root;
    }

    public void setStage(Stage stage) {
        this.stage = stage;
    }

    public User user() {
        return user;
    }

    /** Права текущего пользователя. Для гостя набор прав пуст. */
    public RolePermissions permissions() {
        return RolePermissions.of(user == null ? null : user.role());
    }

    public boolean can(Permission permission) {
        return permissions().can(permission);
    }

    public void setUser(User user) {
        this.user = user;
    }

    public void setLogoutHandler(Runnable logoutHandler) {
        this.logoutHandler = logoutHandler;
    }

    public void logout() {
        user = null;
        if (logoutHandler != null) {
            logoutHandler.run();
        }
    }

    /**
     * Выполняет операцию с БД в фоне, результат передаёт в FX-поток.
     */
    public <T> void async(Callable<T> task, Consumer<T> onSuccess, Consumer<Throwable> onError) {
        worker.submit(() -> {
            try {
                T result = task.call();
                Platform.runLater(() -> onSuccess.accept(result));
            } catch (Throwable error) {
                Throwable cause = unwrap(error);
                if (!worker.isShutdown()) {
                    Platform.runLater(() -> onError.accept(cause));
                }
            }
        });
    }

    public void shutdown() {
        worker.shutdownNow();
        try {
            worker.awaitTermination(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        } catch (InterruptedException exception) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
    }

    public static String errorText(Throwable error) {
        Throwable cause = unwrap(error);
        String message = cause.getMessage();
        if (message == null || message.isBlank()) {
            message = cause.getClass().getSimpleName();
        }
        return message;
    }

    private static Throwable unwrap(Throwable error) {
        Throwable cause = error;
        while (cause.getCause() != null
                && (cause instanceof java.util.concurrent.ExecutionException
                || cause instanceof java.util.concurrent.CompletionException)) {
            cause = cause.getCause();
        }
        return cause;
    }
}

``java
package org.example.sto.ui;

import javafx.application.Application;
import org.example.sto.config.AppConfig;
import org.example.sto.config.DbSettings;
import org.example.sto.db.ConnectionManager;

/**
 * Точка входа JavaFX-приложения.
 *
 * <p>Используется и из основного {@code StoApplication}, и напрямую
 * через {@code mvn javafx:run}.
 */
public final class UiLauncher {

    private static AppContext context;

    private UiLauncher() {
    }

    public static void launch(AppContext appContext) {
        context = appContext;
        Application.launch(StoFxApp.class);
    }

    static AppContext context() {
        return context;
    }

    /**
     * Запуск для javafx-maven-plugin: конфигурация и пул создаются здесь.
     */
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        DbSettings settings;
        try {
            settings = AppConfig.load().dbSettings();
        } catch (RuntimeException error) {
            System.err.println("Ошибка конфигурации: " + error.getMessage());
            System.exit(2);
            return;
        }

        try (ConnectionManager connectionManager = new ConnectionManager(settings)) {
            launch(AppContext.create(connectionManager));
        } catch (RuntimeException error) {
            System.err.println("Не удалось запустить интерфейс: " + error.getMessage());
            error.printStackTrace(System.err);
            System.exit(1);
        }
    }
}
``

## 52. `src/main/java/org/example/sto/ui/StoFxApp.java`

Строк: 136

``java
package org.example.sto.ui;

import javafx.application.Application;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.ProgressIndicator;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.StackPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.scene.paint.Color;
import javafx.stage.Stage;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.Icon;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.ToastHost;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.Ui;
import org.example.sto.ui.pages.LoginView;

/**
 * JavaFX-приложение: подключение к БД, вход и основное окно.
 */
public final class StoFxApp extends Application {

    private AppContext context;
    private StackPane root;
    private ToastHost toast;

    @Override
    public void start(Stage stage) {
        context = UiLauncher.context();
        if (context == null) {
            Platform.exit();
            return;
        }
        context.setStage(stage);
        toast = context.toast();
        context.setLogoutHandler(this::showLogin);

        root = new StackPane();
        root.getStyleClass().add("app-root");

        Scene scene = new Scene(root, 1280, 820);
        String css = StoFxApp.class.getResource("/css/sto.css").toExternalForm();
        scene.getStylesheets().add(css);

        stage.setTitle("СТО · управление автосервисом");
        stage.setScene(scene);
        stage.setMinWidth(1120);
        stage.setMinHeight(720);
        stage.setOnCloseRequest(event -> context.shutdown());
        stage.show();
        stage.centerOnScreen();

        showLoading();
        context.async(context::ping, version -> showLogin(), this::showDatabaseError);
    }

    private void showLoading() {
        ProgressIndicator indicator = new ProgressIndicator();
        indicator.setPrefSize(42, 42);
        Label title = Ui.label("Подключение к PostgreSQL", "section-title");
        Label sub = Ui.label(context.dbSettings().toSafeString(), "page-sub");
        sub.setWrapText(true);
        sub.setMaxWidth(430);
        Label hint = Ui.label("Проверяем доступность контейнера и пула соединений…", "hint");

        VBox card = new VBox(12, indicator, title, sub, hint);
        card.getStyleClass().add("loading-card");
        card.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);

        StackPane wrapper = new StackPane(card);
        wrapper.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        setContent(wrapper);
    }

    private void showDatabaseError(Throwable error) {
        Label icon = new Label();
        icon.getStyleClass().add("empty-icon");
        icon.setGraphic(Icon.create("database", 22, Color.web("#ef4444")));

        Label title = Ui.label("Нет подключения к базе данных", "page-title");
        Label message = Ui.label(AppContext.errorText(error), "page-sub");
        message.setWrapText(true);
        message.setMaxWidth(500);
        Label hint = Ui.label(
                "Проверьте, что контейнер запущен: docker compose up -d. "
                        + "Параметры подключения задаются в application.properties "
                        + "или переменными STO_DB_URL, STO_DB_USERNAME, STO_DB_PASSWORD.",
                "hint");
        hint.setWrapText(true);
        hint.setMaxWidth(500);

        Button retry = Ui.primary("Повторить подключение", Icon.create("refresh", 15, Color.WHITE));
        retry.setOnAction(event -> {
            showLoading();
            context.async(context::ping, version -> showLogin(), this::showDatabaseError);
        });
        Button exit = Ui.ghost("Закрыть приложение", null);
        exit.setOnAction(event -> Platform.exit());

        HBox actions = new HBox(9, retry, exit);
        actions.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        VBox card = new VBox(12, icon, title, message, hint, actions);
        card.getStyleClass().add("login-card");
        card.setMaxWidth(560);
        StackPane wrapper = new StackPane(card);
        wrapper.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        wrapper.setPadding(new javafx.geometry.Insets(40));
        setContent(wrapper);
    }

    private void showLogin() {
        LoginView login = new LoginView(context, this::showShell);
        setContent(login);
    }

    private void showShell() {
        Shell shell = new Shell(context);
        setContent(shell);
    }

    private void setContent(Node node) {
        root.getChildren().setAll(node, toast);
        StackPane.setAlignment(toast, Pos.BOTTOM_RIGHT);
    }

    @Override
    public void stop() {
        if (context != null) {
            context.shutdown();
        }
    }
}
``

## 53. `src/main/java/org/example/sto/ui/Shell.java`

Строк: 87

``java
package org.example.sto.ui;

import javafx.scene.layout.BorderPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.StackPane;
import org.example.sto.model.Permission;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.Sidebar;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.TopBar;
import org.example.sto.ui.pages.CarsView;
import org.example.sto.ui.pages.ClientsView;
import org.example.sto.ui.pages.DashboardView;
import org.example.sto.ui.pages.MastersView;
import org.example.sto.ui.pages.OrdersView;
import org.example.sto.ui.pages.PageView;
import org.example.sto.ui.pages.ServicesView;
import org.example.sto.ui.pages.StatusLogView;

import java.util.EnumMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Основное окно: боковое меню, верхняя панель и область текущего раздела.
 * Набор доступных разделов зависит от роли пользователя.
 */
public final class Shell extends BorderPane {

    private final Map<Navigation, PageView> pages = new EnumMap<>(Navigation.class);
    private final Sidebar sidebar;
    private final TopBar topBar;
    private final StackPane content = new StackPane();
    private final AppContext context;

    public Shell(AppContext context) {
        this.context = context;
        sidebar = new Sidebar(context, this::navigate);
        topBar = new TopBar(this::searchOrders);

        pages.put(Navigation.DASHBOARD, new DashboardView(context, this::openOrder));
        pages.put(Navigation.ORDERS, new OrdersView(context));
        pages.put(Navigation.CLIENTS, new ClientsView(context, this::newOrderForClient));
        pages.put(Navigation.CARS, new CarsView(context));
        pages.put(Navigation.SERVICES, new ServicesView(context));
        pages.put(Navigation.MASTERS, new MastersView(context));
        pages.put(Navigation.LOG, new StatusLogView(context));

        setLeft(sidebar);
        setTop(topBar);
        setCenter(content);
        navigate(firstAvailable());
    }

    private Navigation firstAvailable() {
        Navigation first = Navigation.firstVisibleFor(context.permissions());
        return first == null ? Navigation.SERVICES : first;
    }

    private void navigate(Navigation navigation) {
        if (!context.can(navigation.permission())) {
            return;
        }
        PageView page = pages.get(navigation);
        if (page == null) {
            return;
        }
        topBar.setMeta(navigation);
        sidebar.setActive(navigation);
        content.getChildren().setAll(page.node());
        page.onShow();
    }

    private void openOrder(int orderId) {
        navigate(Navigation.ORDERS);
        ((OrdersView) pages.get(Navigation.ORDERS)).openById(orderId);
    }

    private void newOrderForClient(int clientId) {
        if (!context.can(Permission.CREATE_ORDER)) {
            return;
        }
        navigate(Navigation.ORDERS);
        ((OrdersView) pages.get(Navigation.ORDERS)).openCreate(clientId);
    }

    private void searchOrders(String query) {
        navigate(Navigation.ORDERS);
        ((OrdersView) pages.get(Navigation.ORDERS)).applySearch(query);
    }
}
``

``java
package org.example.sto.ui;

import org.example.sto.model.Permission;
import org.example.sto.model.RolePermissions;

import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Разделы основного окна. Каждому разделу соответствует право доступа:
 * боковое меню и навигация показывают только разрешённые роли пункты.
 */
public enum Navigation {
    DASHBOARD("Обзор", "Главные показатели и события сервиса", "dashboard", Permission.VIEW_DASHBOARD),
    ORDERS("Заявки", "Приём, сопровождение и закрытие заказов", "orders", Permission.VIEW_ORDERS),
    CLIENTS("Клиенты", "База владельцев автомобилей", "clients", Permission.VIEW_CLIENTS),
    CARS("Автомобили", "Автопарк клиентов сервиса", "car", Permission.VIEW_CARS),
    SERVICES("Услуги", "Прайс-лист и длительность работ", "services", Permission.VIEW_SERVICES),
    MASTERS("Мастера", "Команда и специализации", "masters", Permission.VIEW_MASTERS),
    LOG("Журнал статусов", "История изменений, записанная триггерами БД", "history", Permission.VIEW_LOG);

    private final String title;
    private final String subtitle;
    private final String icon;
    private final Permission permission;

    Navigation(String title, String subtitle, String icon, Permission permission) {
        this.title = title;
        this.subtitle = subtitle;
        this.icon = icon;
        this.permission = permission;
    }

    public String title() {
        return title;
    }

    public String subtitle() {
        return subtitle;
    }

    public String icon() {
        return icon;
    }

    public Permission permission() {
        return permission;
    }

    /**
     * Разделы, доступные роли. Используется боковым меню и навигацией,
     * поэтому проверка прав выполняется в одном месте.
     */
    public static List<Navigation> visibleFor(RolePermissions permissions) {
        return Arrays.stream(values())
                .filter(navigation -> permissions.can(navigation.permission()))
                .toList();
    }

    /** Первый доступный раздел — с него открывается основное окно. */
    public static Navigation firstVisibleFor(RolePermissions permissions) {
        return visibleFor(permissions).stream().findFirst().orElse(null);
    }
}
``

## 55. `src/main/java/org/example/sto/ui/components/DonutChart.java`

Строк: 79

``java
package org.example.sto.ui.components;

import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Group;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.layout.StackPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.scene.paint.Color;
import javafx.scene.shape.Arc;
import javafx.scene.shape.ArcType;
import javafx.scene.shape.Circle;
import javafx.scene.shape.StrokeLineCap;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Кольцевая диаграмма распределения заявок по статусам.
 */
public final class DonutChart extends StackPane {

    public record Slice(String name, long count, Color color) {
    }

    private final Group segments = new Group();
    private final Label total = new Label("0");
    private final Label caption = new Label("заявок");

    public DonutChart() {
        setMinSize(190, 190);
        setPrefSize(190, 190);
        setMaxSize(190, 190);

        total.setStyle("-fx-font-size: 27px; -fx-font-weight: bold; -fx-text-fill: #0f172a;");
        caption.getStyleClass().add("muted");
        VBox center = new VBox(1, total, caption);
        center.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);

        getChildren().addAll(segments, center);
        setData(List.of());
    }

    public void setData(List<Slice> slices) {
        segments.getChildren().clear();

        Circle background = new Circle(0, 0, 68);
        background.setFill(Color.TRANSPARENT);
        background.setStroke(Color.web("#eef2f8"));
        background.setStrokeWidth(20);
        segments.getChildren().add(background);

        long sum = slices.stream().mapToLong(Slice::count).sum();
        total.setText(String.valueOf(sum));
        caption.setText(org.example.sto.ui.util.Fmt.pluralWord(sum, "заявка", "заявки", "заявок"));
        if (sum == 0) {
            return;
        }

        double consumed = 0;
        for (Slice slice : slices) {
            if (slice.count() <= 0) {
                continue;
            }
            double fraction = slice.count() / (double) sum;
            double start = 90 - consumed * 360;
            double extent = -fraction * 360;
            if (slices.stream().filter(item -> item.count() > 0).count() > 1) {
                extent += 1.2;
            }
            Arc arc = new Arc(0, 0, 68, 68, start, extent);
            arc.setType(ArcType.OPEN);
            arc.setFill(Color.TRANSPARENT);
            arc.setStroke(slice.color());
            arc.setStrokeWidth(20);
            arc.setStrokeLineCap(StrokeLineCap.BUTT);
            segments.getChildren().add(arc);
            consumed += fraction;
        }
    }
}
``

            Map.entry("user", "M20 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-4-4H8a4 4 0 0 0-4 4v2M12 11a4 4 0 1 0 0-8 4 4 0 0 0 0 8"),
            Map.entry("refresh", "M23 4v6h-6M1 20v-6h6M3.5 9a9 9 0 0 1 14.8-3.4L23 10M1 14l4.7 4.4A9 9 0 0 0 20.5 15"),
            Map.entry("power", "M12 2v10M18.4 6.6a9 9 0 1 1-12.8 0")
    );

    private static final Color DEFAULT_COLOR = Color.web("#64748b");

    private Icon() {
    }

    public static StackPane create(String name, double size) {
        return create(name, size, DEFAULT_COLOR);
    }

    public static StackPane create(String name, double size, Color color) {
        SVGPath path = new SVGPath();
        path.setContent(PATHS.getOrDefault(name, ""));
        path.setFill(Color.TRANSPARENT);
        path.setStroke(color);
        path.setStrokeWidth(1.8);
        path.setStrokeLineCap(StrokeLineCap.ROUND);
        path.setStrokeLineJoin(StrokeLineJoin.ROUND);

        double scale = size / 24.0;
        path.getTransforms().add(new Scale(scale, scale));
        Group group = new Group(path);

        StackPane holder = new StackPane(group);
        holder.setMinSize(size, size);
        holder.setPrefSize(size, size);
        holder.setMaxSize(size, size);
        holder.setMouseTransparent(true);
        return holder;
    }

    public static void recolor(Node iconHolder, Color color) {
        if (iconHolder instanceof StackPane holder && !holder.getChildren().isEmpty()
                && holder.getChildren().get(0) instanceof Group group
                && !group.getChildren().isEmpty()
                && group.getChildren().get(0) instanceof SVGPath path) {
            path.setStroke(color);
        }
    }
}
``

package org.example.sto.ui.components;

import javafx.event.EventHandler;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.ScrollPane;
import javafx.scene.input.KeyCode;
import javafx.scene.input.KeyEvent;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.Region;
import javafx.scene.layout.StackPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;

/**
 * Модальное окно с затемнением фона и закрытием по Esc.
 */
public final class Modal extends StackPane {

    private final VBox card = new VBox(14);
    private final VBox content = new VBox(14);
    private final HBox footer = new HBox(9);
    private final ScrollPane scroll = new ScrollPane(content);

    private StackPane host;
    private EventHandler<KeyEvent> escapeHandler;
    private Runnable onHide;

    public Modal(String title, String subtitle, double width) {
        getStyleClass().add("modal-overlay");
        setMaxSize(Double.MAX_VALUE, Double.MAX_VALUE);

        Label titleLabel = Ui.label(title, "modal-title");
        Label subLabel = Ui.label(subtitle == null ? "" : subtitle, "modal-sub");
        Ui.setShown(subLabel, subtitle != null && !subtitle.isBlank());
        VBox headText = new VBox(2, titleLabel, subLabel);

        Button close = Ui.iconButton("close", "Закрыть");
        close.setOnAction(event -> hide());
        HBox header = new HBox(12, headText, Ui.spacer(), close);
        header.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        content.setFillWidth(true);
        scroll.setFitToWidth(true);
        scroll.getStyleClass().add("modal-scroll");
        scroll.setMaxHeight(560);
        scroll.setHbarPolicy(ScrollPane.ScrollBarPolicy.NEVER);
        VBox.setVgrow(scroll, javafx.scene.layout.Priority.ALWAYS);

        footer.getStyleClass().add("modal-footer");
        footer.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_RIGHT);

        card.getStyleClass().add("modal-card");
        card.setPrefWidth(width);
        card.setMaxWidth(width);
        card.setMaxHeight(Region.USE_PREF_SIZE);
        card.getChildren().addAll(header, scroll);

        setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        getChildren().add(card);
    }

    public Modal setContent(Node node) {
        content.getChildren().setAll(node);
        return this;
    }

    public Modal setFooter(Node... nodes) {
        footer.getChildren().setAll(nodes);
        if (!card.getChildren().contains(footer) && nodes.length > 0) {
            card.getChildren().add(footer);
        }
        if (nodes.length == 0) {
            card.getChildren().remove(footer);
        }
        return this;
    }

    public Modal onHide(Runnable onHide) {
        this.onHide = onHide;
        return this;
    }

    public void show(StackPane host) {
        this.host = host;
        if (!host.getChildren().contains(this)) {
            host.getChildren().add(this);
        }
        if (host.getScene() != null) {
            escapeHandler = event -> {
                if (event.getCode() == KeyCode.ESCAPE) {
                    hide();
                }
            };
            host.getScene().addEventFilter(KeyEvent.KEY_PRESSED, escapeHandler);
        }
        requestFocus();
    }

    public void hide() {
        if (host != null && host.getScene() != null && escapeHandler != null) {
            host.getScene().removeEventFilter(KeyEvent.KEY_PRESSED, escapeHandler);
        }
        escapeHandler = null;
        if (host != null) {
            host.getChildren().remove(this);
        }
        if (onHide != null) {
            onHide.run();
        }
    }

    public static Modal confirm(StackPane host, String title, String message,
                                String confirmText, Runnable action) {
        Modal modal = new Modal(title, "Действие нельзя отменить", 440);
        Label text = new Label(message);
        text.setWrapText(true);
        text.getStyleClass().add("page-sub");
        modal.setContent(text);

        Button cancel = Ui.ghost("Отмена", null);
        cancel.setOnAction(event -> modal.hide());
        Button confirm = Ui.danger(confirmText, null);
        confirm.setOnAction(event -> {
            modal.hide();
            action.run();
        });
        modal.setFooter(cancel, confirm);
        modal.show(host);
        return modal;
    }
}
``

package org.example.sto.ui.components;

import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.Priority;
import javafx.scene.layout.StackPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.scene.paint.Color;
import org.example.sto.model.User;
import org.example.sto.ui.AppContext;
import org.example.sto.ui.Navigation;
import org.example.sto.ui.util.Fmt;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

/**
 * Тёмное боковое меню с профилем пользователя.
 */
public final class Sidebar extends VBox {

    private record NavItem(Navigation navigation, Button button, StackPane icon) {
    }

    private final AppContext context;
    private final List<NavItem> items = new ArrayList<>();

    public Sidebar(AppContext context, Consumer<Navigation> onNavigate) {
        this.context = context;
        getStyleClass().add("sidebar");

        Label logoBadge = new Label();
        logoBadge.getStyleClass().add("brand-badge");
        logoBadge.setGraphic(Icon.create("car", 22, Color.WHITE));
        VBox brandText = new VBox(1,
                Ui.label("СТО · CRM", "brand-name"),
                Ui.label("управление автосервисом", "brand-caption"));
        HBox brand = new HBox(11, logoBadge, brandText);
        brand.getStyleClass().add("brand");
        brand.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        VBox nav = new VBox(4, Ui.label("РАЗДЕЛЫ", "nav-title"));
        nav.getStyleClass().add("nav");
        for (Navigation navigation : Navigation.visibleFor(context.permissions())) {
            StackPane icon = Icon.create(navigation.icon(), 18, Color.web("#aab8cc"));
            Button button = new Button(navigation.title());
            button.setGraphic(icon);
            button.getStyleClass().add("nav-btn");
            button.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
            button.setOnAction(event -> onNavigate.accept(navigation));
            items.add(new NavItem(navigation, button, icon));
            nav.getChildren().add(button);
        }

        User user = context.user();
        Label avatar = new Label(user == null ? "?" : Fmt.initials(user.fullName()));
        avatar.getStyleClass().addAll("avatar", "avatar-dark");
        VBox userText = new VBox(1,
                Ui.label(user == null ? "Гость" : user.fullName(), "user-name"),
                Ui.label(user == null ? "—" : Fmt.role(user.role()), "user-role"));
        Button logout = new Button();
        logout.getStyleClass().add("logout-btn");
        logout.setGraphic(Icon.create("logout", 17, Color.web("#8798b0")));
        logout.setTooltip(new javafx.scene.control.Tooltip("Выйти из аккаунта"));
        logout.setOnAction(event -> context.logout());

        HBox userCard = new HBox(9, avatar, userText, Ui.spacer(), logout);
        userCard.getStyleClass().add("user-card");
        userCard.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        HBox.setHgrow(userText, Priority.ALWAYS);

        VBox footer = new VBox(8,
                Ui.label("PostgreSQL 16 · локальный стенд", "brand-caption"),
                userCard);
        footer.getStyleClass().add("sidebar-footer");

        getChildren().addAll(brand, nav, Ui.spacer(), footer);
        VBox.setVgrow(nav, Priority.NEVER);
    }

    public void setActive(Navigation active) {
        for (NavItem item : items) {
            boolean selected = item.navigation() == active;
            item.button().getStyleClass().remove("nav-btn-active");
            if (selected) {
                item.button().getStyleClass().add("nav-btn-active");
            }
            Icon.recolor(item.icon(), selected ? Color.WHITE : Color.web("#aab8cc"));
        }
    }
}
``

package org.example.sto.ui.components;

import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.control.TableCell;
import javafx.scene.control.TableColumn;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import org.example.sto.ui.util.Fmt;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.function.Function;

/**
 * Готовые ячейки таблиц: бейджи, суммы, номера и кнопки действий.
 */
public final class TableCells {

    private TableCells() {
    }

    public static <S> TableColumn<S, String> badge(String title, Function<S, String> value, double width) {
        TableColumn<S, String> column = Ui.column(title, value, width);
        column.setCellFactory(ignored -> new TableCell<>() {
            @Override
            protected void updateItem(String item, boolean empty) {
                super.updateItem(item, empty);
                setText(null);
                setGraphic(empty || item == null ? null : StatusBadge.create(item));
            }
        });
        return column;
    }

    public static <S> TableColumn<S, String> chip(String title, Function<S, String> value, double width) {
        TableColumn<S, String> column = Ui.column(title, value, width);
        column.setCellFactory(ignored -> new TableCell<>() {
            @Override
            protected void updateItem(String item, boolean empty) {
                super.updateItem(item, empty);
                setText(null);
                if (empty || item == null) {
                    setGraphic(null);
                } else {
                    Label chip = new Label(item);
                    chip.getStyleClass().addAll("chip", "chip-plate");
                    setGraphic(chip);
                }
            }
        });
        return column;
    }

    public static <S> TableColumn<S, BigDecimal> money(String title, Function<S, BigDecimal> value, double width) {
        TableColumn<S, BigDecimal> column = new TableColumn<>(title);
        column.setCellValueFactory(data -> new javafx.beans.property.ReadOnlyObjectWrapper<>(value.apply(data.getValue())));
        column.setPrefWidth(width);
        column.setStyle("-fx-alignment: CENTER-RIGHT;");
        column.setCellFactory(ignored -> new TableCell<>() {
            @Override
            protected void updateItem(BigDecimal item, boolean empty) {
                super.updateItem(item, empty);
                setText(empty ? null : Fmt.money(item));
                setStyle("-fx-alignment: CENTER-RIGHT; -fx-font-weight: bold;");
            }
        });
        return column;
    }

    public static <S> TableColumn<S, Void> action(String title, Function<S, Node> factory, double width) {
        TableColumn<S, Void> column = new TableColumn<>(title);
        column.setPrefWidth(width);
        column.setSortable(false);
        column.setCellFactory(ignored -> new TableCell<>() {
            @Override
            protected void updateItem(Void item, boolean empty) {
                super.updateItem(item, empty);
                if (empty || getIndex() < 0 || getIndex() >= getTableView().getItems().size()) {
                    setGraphic(null);
                } else {
                    setGraphic(factory.apply(getTableView().getItems().get(getIndex())));
                }
            }
        });
        return column;
    }
}
``

package org.example.sto.ui.components;

import javafx.animation.FadeTransition;
import javafx.animation.PauseTransition;
import javafx.animation.SequentialTransition;
import javafx.animation.TranslateTransition;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.Priority;
import javafx.scene.layout.StackPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.scene.paint.Color;
import javafx.util.Duration;

/**
 * Всплывающие уведомления в правом нижнем углу окна.
 */
public final class ToastHost extends StackPane {

    private final VBox stack = new VBox(9);

    public ToastHost() {
        setPickOnBounds(false);
        setMouseTransparent(true);
        stack.setAlignment(Pos.BOTTOM_RIGHT);
        stack.setPadding(new Insets(0, 24, 22, 0));
        stack.setPickOnBounds(false);
        stack.setMaxSize(StackPane.USE_PREF_SIZE, StackPane.USE_PREF_SIZE);
        StackPane.setAlignment(stack, Pos.BOTTOM_RIGHT);
        getChildren().add(stack);
    }

    public void success(String message) {
        show("success", message, "check", Color.web("#10b981"));
    }

    public void error(String message) {
        show("error", message, "alert", Color.web("#ef4444"));
    }

    public void info(String message) {
        show("info", message, "spark", Color.web("#2463eb"));
    }

    private void show(String type, String message, String icon, Color color) {
        if (message == null || message.isBlank()) {
            return;
        }
        Label text = new Label(message);
        text.getStyleClass().add("toast-text");
        text.setWrapText(true);
        text.setMaxWidth(340);
        HBox.setHgrow(text, Priority.ALWAYS);

        HBox toast = new HBox(10, Icon.create(icon, 18, color), text);
        toast.getStyleClass().addAll("toast", "toast-" + type);
        toast.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        toast.setMaxWidth(420);

        stack.getChildren().add(toast);
        while (stack.getChildren().size() > 4) {
            stack.getChildren().remove(0);
        }

        toast.setOpacity(0);
        toast.setTranslateY(12);
        FadeTransition fadeIn = new FadeTransition(Duration.millis(180), toast);
        fadeIn.setToValue(1);
        TranslateTransition slideIn = new TranslateTransition(Duration.millis(220), toast);
        slideIn.setToY(0);
        fadeIn.play();
        slideIn.play();

        PauseTransition wait = new PauseTransition(Duration.seconds(4.2));
        FadeTransition fadeOut = new FadeTransition(Duration.millis(220), toast);
        fadeOut.setToValue(0);
        SequentialTransition sequence = new SequentialTransition(wait, fadeOut);
        sequence.setOnFinished(event -> stack.getChildren().remove(toast));
        sequence.play();
    }
}
``

package org.example.sto.ui.components;

import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.TextField;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.Priority;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.scene.paint.Color;
import org.example.sto.ui.Navigation;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

/**
 * Верхняя панель: заголовок раздела, дата, поиск и индикатор базы.
 */
public final class TopBar extends HBox {

    private final Label title = Ui.label("Обзор", "page-title");
    private final Label subtitle = Ui.label("", "page-sub");
    private final TextField search = new TextField();
    private final Consumer<String> onSearch;

    public TopBar(Consumer<String> onSearch) {
        this.onSearch = onSearch;
        getStyleClass().add("topbar");

        VBox meta = new VBox(1, title, subtitle);
        meta.getStyleClass().add("topbar-meta");

        search.setPromptText("Номер заявки, клиент, автомобиль…");
        search.getStyleClass().add("search-input");
        search.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        search.setOnAction(event -> {
            String query = search.getText() == null ? "" : search.getText().trim();
            search.clear();
            onSearch.accept(query);
        });
        HBox.setHgrow(search, Priority.ALWAYS);
        HBox searchBox = new HBox(8, Icon.create("search", 15), search);
        searchBox.getStyleClass().add("search-box");
        searchBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        Label date = Ui.label(
                LocalDate.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("d MMMM yyyy", Locale.of("ru", "RU"))),
                "small");
        HBox dateChip = new HBox(7, Icon.create("calendar", 14), date);
        dateChip.getStyleClass().add("date-chip");
        dateChip.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        Label dot = new Label();
        dot.getStyleClass().addAll("db-dot", "db-dot-on");
        HBox dbStatus = new HBox(7, dot, Ui.label("PostgreSQL", "small"));
        dbStatus.getStyleClass().add("db-status");
        dbStatus.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        javafx.scene.control.Tooltip.install(
                dbStatus, new javafx.scene.control.Tooltip("Соединение с базой данных установлено"));

        HBox right = new HBox(10, searchBox, dateChip, dbStatus);
        right.getStyleClass().add("topbar-right");
        right.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_RIGHT);
        HBox.setHgrow(searchBox, Priority.ALWAYS);

        getChildren().addAll(meta, Ui.spacer(), right);
        setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
    }

    public void setMeta(Navigation navigation) {
        title.setText(navigation.title());
        subtitle.setText(navigation.subtitle());
    }
}
``
