``java
package org.example.sto.ui.pages;

import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.ComboBox;
import javafx.scene.control.ContentDisplay;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.ProgressIndicator;
import javafx.scene.control.ScrollPane;
import javafx.scene.control.Spinner;
import javafx.scene.control.TableRow;
import javafx.scene.control.TableView;
import javafx.scene.control.TextArea;
import javafx.scene.control.TextField;
import javafx.scene.control.ToggleButton;
import javafx.scene.control.ToggleGroup;
import javafx.scene.layout.ColumnConstraints;
import javafx.scene.layout.GridPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.Priority;
import javafx.scene.layout.StackPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.scene.paint.Color;
import javafx.util.StringConverter;
import org.example.sto.model.Car;
import org.example.sto.model.Client;
import org.example.sto.model.Master;
import org.example.sto.model.Order;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderServiceItem;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderStatus;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderStatusLogEntry;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderSummary;
import org.example.sto.model.Permission;
import org.example.sto.model.Service;
import org.example.sto.ui.AppContext;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.Icon;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.Modal;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.StatusBadge;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.TableCells;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.Ui;
import org.example.sto.ui.util.Fmt;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Экран заявок: фильтры, создание, карточка, услуги, закрытие и журнал.
 */
public final class OrdersView implements PageView {

    private record OrdersData(
            List<OrderSummary> orders,
            List<Client> clients,
            List<Car> cars,
            List<Service> services,
            List<Master> masters,
            List<OrderStatus> statuses) {
    }

    private record OrderDetail(
            Order order,
            Client client,
            Car car,
            Master master,
            BigDecimal total,
            List<OrderServiceItem> services,
            List<OrderStatusLogEntry> log) {
    }

    private final AppContext context;
    private final ScrollPane root;

    private final TableView<OrderSummary> table = Ui.table();
    private final TextField search = new TextField();
    private final Label foot = Ui.label("", "table-foot");
    private final ToggleGroup filterGroup = new ToggleGroup();
    private final Map<String, Label> tabCounts = new LinkedHashMap<>();

    private OrdersData data = new OrdersData(List.of(), List.of(), List.of(), List.of(), List.of(), List.of());
    private Modal detailModal;
    private int detailId;
    private boolean pendingCreate;
    private Integer pendingClientId;

    public OrdersView(AppContext context) {
        this.context = context;

        Button create = Ui.primary("Новая заявка", Icon.create("plus", 15, Color.WHITE));
        create.setOnAction(event -> openCreate(null));
        Button refresh = Ui.ghost("Обновить", Icon.create("refresh", 15));
        refresh.setOnAction(event -> load());

        List<Node> headerActions = new ArrayList<>();
        headerActions.add(refresh);
        if (context.can(Permission.CREATE_ORDER)) {
            headerActions.add(create);
        }

        HBox tabs = new HBox(3);
        tabs.getStyleClass().add("tab-bar");
        tabs.getChildren().addAll(
                filterTab("Все", "all"),
                filterTab("Новые", "новая"),
                filterTab("В работе", "в работе"),
                filterTab("Завершённые", "завершена"),
                filterTab("Отменённые", "отменена"));
        filterGroup.selectToggle(filterGroup.getToggles().get(0));
        filterGroup.selectedToggleProperty().addListener((obs, old, value) -> applyFilter());

        search.setPromptText("Номер, клиент, автомобиль…");
        search.getStyleClass().add("search-input");
        search.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        HBox.setHgrow(search, Priority.ALWAYS);
        HBox searchBox = new HBox(8, Icon.create("search", 15), search);
        searchBox.getStyleClass().add("search-box");
        searchBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        searchBox.setPrefWidth(290);
        search.textProperty().addListener((obs, old, value) -> applyFilter());

        HBox toolbar = new HBox(14, tabs, Ui.spacer(), searchBox);
        toolbar.getStyleClass().add("card");
        toolbar.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        configureTable();
        VBox tableCard = Ui.card(table, foot);
        VBox.setVgrow(table, Priority.ALWAYS);

        VBox content = Views.page(
                Ui.pageHeader("Рабочий процесс", "Заявки сервиса",
                        "Полный цикл: приём автомобиля, услуги и закрытие с записью в журнал",
                        headerActions.toArray(Node[]::new)),
                toolbar, tableCard);
        root = Views.scroll(content);
    }

    private ToggleButton filterTab(String title, String key) {
        Label count = Ui.label("0", "tab-count");
        ToggleButton tab = new ToggleButton(title);
        tab.setUserData(key);
        tab.setToggleGroup(filterGroup);
        tab.getStyleClass().add("tab");
        tab.setGraphic(count);
        tab.setContentDisplay(ContentDisplay.RIGHT);
        tab.setGraphicTextGap(6);
        tabCounts.put(key, count);
        return tab;
    }

    private void configureTable() {
        table.setPrefHeight(460);
        table.getColumns().add(Ui.column("Заявка", order -> "#" + order.orderId(), 70));
        table.getColumns().add(Ui.column("Клиент", OrderSummary::client, 170));
        table.getColumns().add(TableCells.chip("Автомобиль", OrderSummary::car, 115));
        table.getColumns().add(TableCells.badge("Статус", OrderSummary::status, 100));
        table.getColumns().add(TableCells.money("Стоимость", OrderSummary::totalCost, 110));
        table.getColumns().add(TableCells.action("", order -> {
            Button open = Ui.ghost("Открыть", Icon.create("eye", 14));
            open.getStyleClass().add("btn-sm");
            open.setOnAction(event -> openDetail(order.orderId()));
            return open;
        }, 105));

        table.setRowFactory(ignored -> {
            TableRow<OrderSummary> row = new TableRow<>();
            row.setOnMouseClicked(event -> {
                if (event.getClickCount() == 2 && !row.isEmpty()) {
                    openDetail(row.getItem().orderId());
                }
            });
            return row;
        });
    }

    @Override
    public Node node() {
        return root;
    }

    @Override
    public void onShow() {
        load();
    }

    private void load() {
        context.async(
                () -> new OrdersData(
                        context.orders().listVisible(context.user()),
                        context.catalog().clients(),
                        context.catalog().cars(),
                        context.catalog().services(),
                        context.catalog().masters(),
                        context.catalog().orderStatuses()),
                loaded -> {
                    data = loaded;
                    updateCounts();
                    applyFilter();
                    if (pendingCreate) {
                        pendingCreate = false;
                        Integer clientId = pendingClientId;
                        pendingClientId = null;
                        if (data.clients().isEmpty()) {
                            context.toast().info(
                                    "В базе нет клиентов — сначала добавьте клиента в разделе «Клиенты».");
                        } else {
                            openCreate(clientId);
                        }
                    }
                },
                error -> context.toast().error(AppContext.errorText(error)));
    }

    private void updateCounts() {
        tabCounts.get("all").setText(String.valueOf(data.orders().size()));
        for (String key : List.of("новая", "в работе", "завершена", "отменена")) {
            long count = data.orders().stream().filter(order -> key.equals(order.status())).count();
            tabCounts.get(key).setText(String.valueOf(count));
        }
    }

    private void applyFilter() {
        ToggleButton selected = (ToggleButton) filterGroup.getSelectedToggle();
        String filter = selected == null ? "all" : String.valueOf(selected.getUserData());
        String query = search.getText() == null ? "" : search.getText().trim().toLowerCase();

        List<OrderSummary> filtered = data.orders().stream()
                .filter(order -> "all".equals(filter) || filter.equals(order.status()))
                .filter(order -> query.isBlank()
                        || String.valueOf(order.orderId()).contains(query)
                        || contains(order.client(), query)
                        || contains(order.car(), query)
                        || contains(order.status(), query))
                .toList();

        table.getItems().setAll(filtered);
        foot.setText("Показано " + filtered.size() + " из " + data.orders().size() + " "
                + Fmt.pluralWord(data.orders().size(), "заявки", "заявок", "заявок"));
        if (filtered.isEmpty()) {
            table.setPlaceholder(Ui.empty("orders", "Заявки не найдены",
                    "Измените фильтр или создайте новую заявку."));
        }
    }

    public void applySearch(String query) {
        search.setText(query == null ? "" : query);
        applyFilter();
    }

    public void openById(int orderId) {
        openDetail(orderId);
    }

    public void openCreate(Integer preselectClientId) {
        if (!context.can(Permission.CREATE_ORDER)) {
            context.toast().info("Создавать заявки может администратор или мастер-приёмщик.");
            return;
        }
        if (data.clients().isEmpty()) {
            pendingCreate = true;
            pendingClientId = preselectClientId;
            load();
            return;
        }

        ComboBox<Client> clientCombo = Ui.combo(data.clients(), new StringConverter<>() {
            @Override
            public String toString(Client client) {
                return client == null ? "" : client.fullName() + " · " + client.phone();
            }

            @Override
            public Client fromString(String string) {
                return null;
            }
        }, "Выберите клиента");

        ComboBox<Car> carCombo = Ui.combo(List.of(), new StringConverter<>() {
            @Override
            public String toString(Car car) {
                return car == null ? "" : car.brand() + " " + car.model() + " · " + car.plateNum();
            }

            @Override
            public Car fromString(String string) {
                return null;
            }
        }, "Сначала выберите клиента");

        List<Master> masterItems = new ArrayList<>();
        masterItems.add(new Master(0, 0, "Не назначать", null, true));
        masterItems.addAll(data.masters());
        ComboBox<Master> masterCombo = Ui.combo(masterItems, new StringConverter<>() {
            @Override
            public String toString(Master master) {
                if (master == null) {
                    return "";
                }
                if (master.id() == 0) {
                    return master.fullName();
                }
                return master.fullName() + (master.specialization() == null ? "" : " · " + master.specialization());
            }

            @Override
            public Master fromString(String string) {
                return null;
            }
        }, "Не назначен");
        masterCombo.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();

        TextArea description = new TextArea();
        description.setPromptText("Опишите неисправность или пожелания клиента");
        description.setWrapText(true);
        description.setPrefRowCount(3);

        Label error = Ui.label("", "error-text");
        Ui.setShown(error, false);

        clientCombo.valueProperty().addListener((obs, old, client) -> {
            List<Car> cars = client == null ? List.of() : data.cars().stream()
                    .filter(car -> car.clientId() == client.id())
                    .toList();
            carCombo.getItems().setAll(cars);
            carCombo.setPromptText(cars.isEmpty() ? "У клиента нет автомобилей" : "Выберите автомобиль");
            if (cars.size() == 1) {
                carCombo.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();
            } else {
                carCombo.getSelectionModel().clearSelection();
            }
        });

        if (preselectClientId != null) {
            data.clients().stream()
                    .filter(client -> client.id() == preselectClientId)
                    .findFirst()
                    .ifPresent(clientCombo::setValue);
        }

        Modal modal = new Modal("Новая заявка", "Приём автомобиля на обслуживание", 560);
        Button cancel = Ui.ghost("Отмена", null);
        cancel.setOnAction(event -> modal.hide());
        Button submit = Ui.primary("Создать заявку", Icon.create("plus", 15, Color.WHITE));
        submit.setDefaultButton(true);

        VBox form = new VBox(12,
                Ui.field("Клиент", clientCombo),
                Ui.field("Автомобиль", carCombo),
                Ui.field("Мастер", masterCombo),
                Ui.field("Описание неисправности", description),
                error);
        modal.setContent(form);
        modal.setFooter(cancel, submit);

        submit.setOnAction(event -> {
            Client client = clientCombo.getValue();
            Car car = carCombo.getValue();
            Master master = masterCombo.getValue();
            String text = description.getText() == null ? "" : description.getText().trim();

            if (client == null) {
                Ui.showError(error, "Выберите клиента");
                return;
            }
            if (car == null) {
                Ui.showError(error, "Выберите автомобиль клиента");
                return;
            }
            if (text.isBlank()) {
                Ui.showError(error, "Опишите неисправность");
                return;
            }
            if (text.length() > 2000) {
                Ui.showError(error, "Описание не должно превышать 2000 символов");
                return;
            }

            Integer masterId = master == null || master.id() == 0 ? null : master.id();
            submit.setDisable(true);
            submit.setText("Создание…");
            context.async(
                    () -> context.orders().createOrder(
                            context.user(), client.id(), car.id(), masterId, text),
                    orderId -> {
                        modal.hide();
                        context.toast().success("Заявка #" + orderId + " создана");
                        load();
                        openDetail(orderId);
                    },
                    throwable -> {
                        submit.setDisable(false);
                        submit.setText("Создать заявку");
                        Ui.showError(error, AppContext.errorText(throwable));
                    });
        });

        modal.show(context.overlay());
    }

    private void openDetail(int orderId) {
        if (detailModal != null) {
            detailModal.hide();
        }
        detailId = orderId;
        Modal modal = new Modal("Заявка #" + orderId, "Карточка обращения", 820);
        modal.onHide(() -> {
            if (detailId == orderId) {
                detailModal = null;
            }
        });
        detailModal = modal;

        StackPane loading = new StackPane(new ProgressIndicator());
        loading.setMinHeight(160);
        modal.setContent(loading);
        Button close = Ui.ghost("Закрыть", null);
        close.setOnAction(event -> modal.hide());
        modal.setFooter(close);
        modal.show(context.overlay());
        reloadDetail(orderId);
    }

    private void reloadDetail(int orderId) {
        if (detailModal == null) {
            return;
        }
        StackPane loading = new StackPane(new ProgressIndicator());
        loading.setMinHeight(160);
        detailModal.setContent(loading);
        context.async(
                () -> fetchDetail(orderId),
                detail -> {
                    if (detailModal == null || detailId != orderId) {
                        return;
                    }
                    detailModal.setContent(buildDetail(detail));
                    detailModal.setFooter(buildDetailFooter(detail));
                },
                error -> {
                    if (detailModal != null && detailId == orderId) {
                        detailModal.hide();
                    }
                    context.toast().error(AppContext.errorText(error));
                });
    }

    private OrderDetail fetchDetail(int orderId) {
        Order order = context.orders().getOrderVisible(context.user(), orderId);
        Client client = context.catalog().clientById(order.clientId());
        Car car = context.catalog().carById(order.carId());
        Master master = order.masterId() == null ? null : context.catalog().masters().stream()
                .filter(item -> item.id() == order.masterId())
                .findFirst()
                .orElse(null);
        BigDecimal total = context.orders().calculateTotal(context.user(), orderId);
        List<OrderServiceItem> services = context.orders().orderServices(context.user(), orderId);
        List<OrderStatusLogEntry> log = context.orders().statusLog(context.user(), orderId);
        return new OrderDetail(order, client, car, master, total, services, log);
    }

    private VBox buildDetail(OrderDetail detail) {
        Order order = detail.order();
        Label badge = StatusBadge.create(order.statusName());
        Label total = Ui.label(Fmt.money(detail.total()), "amount-lg");
        HBox summary = new HBox(10, badge, Ui.spacer(), total);
        summary.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        GridPane meta = new GridPane();
        meta.getStyleClass().add("meta-grid");
        ColumnConstraints left = new ColumnConstraints();
        left.setPercentWidth(50);
        ColumnConstraints right = new ColumnConstraints();
        right.setPercentWidth(50);
        meta.getColumnConstraints().addAll(left, right);
        meta.add(metaBox("Клиент", detail.client().fullName()
                + "\n" + detail.client().phone()
                + (detail.client().email() == null ? "" : "\n" + detail.client().email())), 0, 0);
        meta.add(metaBox("Автомобиль", detail.car().brand() + " " + detail.car().model()
                + "\n" + detail.car().plateNum()
                + (detail.car().vin() == null ? "" : "\nVIN " + detail.car().vin())), 1, 0);
        meta.add(metaBox("Мастер", detail.master() == null ? "Не назначен" : detail.master().fullName()), 0, 1);
        meta.add(metaBox("Создана", Fmt.dateTime(order.createdAt())), 1, 1);
        meta.add(metaBox("Закрыта", order.closedAt() == null ? "—" : Fmt.dateTime(order.closedAt())), 0, 2);
        meta.add(metaBox("Итоговая сумма", Fmt.money(detail.total())), 1, 2);

        Label description = Ui.label(Fmt.nullSafe(order.description()), "small");
        description.setWrapText(true);
        VBox descriptionCard = Ui.card("Описание неисправности", null, description);

        TableView<OrderServiceItem> servicesTable = Ui.table();
        servicesTable.setPrefHeight(160);
        servicesTable.getColumns().add(Ui.column("Услуга", OrderServiceItem::serviceName, 260));
        servicesTable.getColumns().add(Ui.column("Кол-во", item -> String.valueOf(item.quantity()), 65));
        servicesTable.getColumns().add(TableCells.money("Цена", OrderServiceItem::priceAt, 95));
        servicesTable.getColumns().add(TableCells.money("Сумма", OrderServiceItem::lineTotal, 100));
        servicesTable.getItems().setAll(detail.services());
        servicesTable.setPlaceholder(Ui.empty("services", "Услуг пока нет",
                "Добавьте первую услугу в заявку."));

        boolean editable = !"завершена".equals(order.statusName()) && !"отменена".equals(order.statusName());
        VBox servicesBody = new VBox(10, servicesTable);
        if (editable && context.can(Permission.ADD_ORDER_SERVICE)) {
            servicesBody.getChildren().add(buildAddServiceRow(detail));
        }
        VBox servicesCard = Ui.card("Услуги и стоимость", "Цена фиксируется в момент добавления", servicesBody);
        VBox.setVgrow(servicesTable, Priority.ALWAYS);

        VBox logBox = new VBox(7);
        if (detail.log().isEmpty()) {
            logBox.getChildren().add(Ui.label("Изменений статуса пока не было.", "hint"));
        } else {
            for (OrderStatusLogEntry entry : detail.log()) {
                Label dot = new Label();
                dot.getStyleClass().add("timeline-dot");
                Label text = Ui.label(
                        Fmt.dateTime(entry.changedAt()) + " · "
                                + statusName(entry.oldStatusId()) + " → " + statusName(entry.newStatusId()),
                        "small");
                Label author = Ui.label(Fmt.nullSafe(entry.changedBy()), "timeline-meta");
                HBox row = new HBox(9, dot, text, Ui.spacer(), author);
                row.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
                logBox.getChildren().add(row);
            }
        }
        VBox logCard = Ui.card("Журнал заявки", "Изменения, записанные триггерами БД", logBox);

        return new VBox(14, summary, meta, descriptionCard, servicesCard, logCard);
    }

    private VBox buildAddServiceRow(OrderDetail detail) {
        ComboBox<Service> serviceCombo = Ui.combo(data.services(), new StringConverter<>() {
            @Override
            public String toString(Service service) {
                return service == null ? "" : service.name() + " · " + Fmt.money(service.price());
            }

            @Override
            public Service fromString(String string) {
                return null;
            }
        }, "Выберите услугу");
        HBox.setHgrow(serviceCombo, Priority.ALWAYS);
        serviceCombo.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);

        Spinner<Integer> quantity = new Spinner<>(1, 100, 1);
        quantity.setPrefWidth(78);
        quantity.setEditable(false);

        Button add = Ui.primary("Добавить", Icon.create("plus", 14, Color.WHITE));
        add.getStyleClass().add("btn-sm");

        Label error = Ui.label("", "error-text");
        Ui.setShown(error, false);

        add.setOnAction(event -> {
            Service service = serviceCombo.getValue();
            if (service == null) {
                Ui.showError(error, "Выберите услугу");
                return;
            }
            add.setDisable(true);
            context.async(
                    () -> {
                        context.orders().addService(
                                context.user(), detail.order().id(), service.id(), quantity.getValue());
                        return null;
                    },
                    ignored -> {
                        context.toast().success("Услуга «" + service.name() + "» добавлена");
                        load();
                        reloadDetail(detail.order().id());
                    },
                    throwable -> {
                        add.setDisable(false);
                        Ui.showError(error, AppContext.errorText(throwable));
                    });
        });

        HBox row = new HBox(8, serviceCombo, quantity, add);
        row.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        return new VBox(6, row, error);
    }

    private HBox buildDetailFooter(OrderDetail detail) {
        Button close = Ui.ghost("Закрыть", null);
        close.setOnAction(event -> {
            if (detailModal != null) {
                detailModal.hide();
            }
        });

        boolean editable = !"завершена".equals(detail.order().statusName())
                && !"отменена".equals(detail.order().statusName());
        if (!editable || !context.can(Permission.CLOSE_ORDER)) {
            return new HBox(9, close);
        }

        Button finish = Ui.danger("Завершить заявку", Icon.create("check", 15));
        finish.setOnAction(event -> Modal.confirm(
                context.overlay(),
                "Завершить заявку #" + detail.order().id() + "?",
                "Итоговая сумма составит " + Fmt.money(detail.total())
                        + ". После закрытия добавить услуги будет нельзя.",
                "Завершить",
                () -> closeOrder(detail.order().id())));
        return new HBox(9, close, finish);
    }

    private void closeOrder(int orderId) {
        context.async(
                () -> {
                    context.orders().closeOrder(context.user(), orderId);
                    return null;
                },
                ignored -> {
                    context.toast().success("Заявка #" + orderId + " завершена");
                    load();
                    reloadDetail(orderId);
                },
                error -> context.toast().error(AppContext.errorText(error)));
    }

    private VBox metaBox(String label, String value) {
        Label valueLabel = Ui.label(value, "meta-value");
        valueLabel.setWrapText(true);
        return new VBox(2, Ui.label(label, "meta-label"), valueLabel);
    }

    private String statusName(Integer statusId) {
        if (statusId == null) {
            return "—";
        }
        return data.statuses().stream()
                .filter(status -> status.id() == statusId)
                .map(OrderStatus::name)
                .findFirst()
                .orElse("—");
    }

    private static boolean contains(String value, String query) {
        return value != null && value.toLowerCase().contains(query);
    }
}
``

``java
package org.example.sto.ui.pages;

import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.ScrollPane;
import javafx.scene.control.TableRow;
import javafx.scene.control.TableView;
import javafx.scene.control.TextField;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.Priority;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.scene.paint.Color;
import org.example.sto.model.Permission;
import org.example.sto.model.Service;
import org.example.sto.ui.AppContext;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.Icon;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.Modal;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.StatCard;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.TableCells;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.Ui;
import org.example.sto.ui.util.Fmt;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.math.RoundingMode;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * Прайс-лист услуг сервиса.
 */
public final class ServicesView implements PageView {

    private final AppContext context;
    private final ScrollPane root;

    private final TableView<Service> table = Ui.table();
    private final TextField search = new TextField();
    private final Label foot = Ui.label("", "table-foot");
    private final StatCard countCard = new StatCard("Услуг в прайсе", "активных позиций", "services", "#2463eb", "#e8efff");
    private final StatCard averageCard = new StatCard("Средняя цена", "по прайс-листу", "ruble", "#10b981", "#e7f8f1");
    private final StatCard durationCard = new StatCard("Средняя длительность", "минут на услугу", "clock", "#f59e0b", "#fef5e3");

    private List<Service> services = List.of();

    public ServicesView(AppContext context) {
        this.context = context;

        Button refresh = Ui.ghost("Обновить", Icon.create("refresh", 15));
        refresh.setOnAction(event -> load());
        Button create = Ui.primary("Новая услуга", Icon.create("plus", 15, Color.WHITE));
        create.setOnAction(event -> openCreate());

        List<Node> headerActions = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
        headerActions.add(refresh);
        if (context.can(Permission.CREATE_SERVICE)) {
            headerActions.add(create);
        }

        search.setPromptText("Название услуги…");
        search.getStyleClass().add("search-input");
        search.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        HBox.setHgrow(search, Priority.ALWAYS);
        HBox searchBox = new HBox(8, Icon.create("search", 15), search);
        searchBox.getStyleClass().add("search-box");
        searchBox.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        searchBox.setPrefWidth(320);
        search.textProperty().addListener((obs, old, value) -> applyFilter());

        HBox toolbar = new HBox(12, Ui.label("Прайс-лист", "section-title"), Ui.spacer(), searchBox);
        toolbar.getStyleClass().add("card");
        toolbar.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        configureTable();
        VBox tableCard = Ui.card(table, foot);
        VBox.setVgrow(table, Priority.ALWAYS);

        HBox stats = new HBox(14, countCard, averageCard, durationCard);
        HBox.setHgrow(countCard, Priority.ALWAYS);
        HBox.setHgrow(averageCard, Priority.ALWAYS);
        HBox.setHgrow(durationCard, Priority.ALWAYS);

        VBox content = Views.page(
                Ui.pageHeader("Справочник", "Услуги", "Прайс-лист и длительность работ",
                        headerActions.toArray(Node[]::new)),
                stats, toolbar, tableCard);
        root = Views.scroll(content);
    }

    private void configureTable() {
        table.setPrefHeight(420);
        table.getColumns().add(Ui.column("Услуга", Service::name, 340));
        table.getColumns().add(Ui.column("Длительность", service ->
                service.durationMin() == null ? "—" : service.durationMin() + " мин", 140));
        table.getColumns().add(TableCells.money("Стоимость", Service::price, 130));

        table.setRowFactory(ignored -> {
            TableRow<Service> row = new TableRow<>();
            row.setOnMouseClicked(event -> {
                if (event.getClickCount() == 2 && !row.isEmpty()) {
                    openCreate();
                }
            });
            return row;
        });
    }

    @Override
    public Node node() {
        return root;
    }

    @Override
    public void onShow() {
        load();
    }

    private void load() {
        context.async(
                context.catalog()::services,
                loaded -> {
                    services = loaded;
                    renderStats();
                    applyFilter();
                },
                error -> context.toast().error(AppContext.errorText(error)));
    }

    private void renderStats() {
        countCard.setValue(String.valueOf(services.size()));
        BigDecimal average = services.isEmpty()
                ? BigDecimal.ZERO
                : services.stream()
                .map(Service::price)
                .reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add)
                .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(services.size()), 0, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
        averageCard.setValue(Fmt.money(average));
        double avgDuration = services.stream()
                .filter(service -> service.durationMin() != null)
                .mapToInt(Service::durationMin)
                .average()
                .orElse(0);
        durationCard.setValue(Math.round(avgDuration) + " мин");
    }

    private void applyFilter() {
        String query = search.getText() == null ? "" : search.getText().trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        List<Service> filtered = services.stream()
                .filter(service -> query.isBlank() || contains(service.name(), query))
                .toList();
        table.getItems().setAll(filtered);
        foot.setText("Показано " + filtered.size() + " из " + services.size() + " "
                + Fmt.pluralWord(services.size(), "услуги", "услуг", "услуг"));
        if (filtered.isEmpty()) {
            table.setPlaceholder(Ui.empty("services", "Услуги не найдены",
                    "Измените запрос или добавьте новую услугу."));
        }
    }

    private void openCreate() {
        if (!context.can(Permission.CREATE_SERVICE)) {
            context.toast().info("Прайс-лист изменяет только администратор.");
            return;
        }
        TextField name = Ui.input("Например, компьютерная диагностика");
        TextField price = Ui.input("2500");
        TextField duration = Ui.input("45");
        Label error = Ui.label("", "error-text");
        Ui.setShown(error, false);

        Modal modal = new Modal("Новая услуга", "Добавление позиции в прайс-лист", 500);
        Button cancel = Ui.ghost("Отмена", null);
        cancel.setOnAction(event -> modal.hide());
        Button submit = Ui.primary("Добавить услугу", Icon.create("plus", 14, Color.WHITE));
        submit.setDefaultButton(true);

        HBox priceRow = new HBox(12, Ui.field("Стоимость, ₽", price), Ui.field("Длительность, мин", duration));
        HBox.setHgrow(priceRow.getChildren().get(0), Priority.ALWAYS);
        HBox.setHgrow(priceRow.getChildren().get(1), Priority.ALWAYS);

        VBox form = new VBox(12,
                Ui.field("Наименование", name),
                priceRow,
                error);
        modal.setContent(form);
        modal.setFooter(cancel, submit);
        modal.show(context.overlay());

        submit.setOnAction(event -> {
            String serviceName = name.getText() == null ? "" : name.getText().trim();
            if (serviceName.isBlank()) {
                Ui.showError(error, "Укажите наименование услуги");
                return;
            }
            BigDecimal servicePrice;
            try {
                servicePrice = Fmt.parseMoney(price.getText());
            } catch (NumberFormatException exception) {
                Ui.showError(error, "Стоимость должна быть числом");
                return;
            }
            if (servicePrice == null || servicePrice.signum() < 0) {
                Ui.showError(error, "Укажите неотрицательную стоимость");
                return;
            }
            Integer durationValue;
            try {
                durationValue = Fmt.parseInteger(duration.getText());
            } catch (NumberFormatException exception) {
                Ui.showError(error, "Длительность должна быть целым числом");
                return;
            }
            if (durationValue != null && durationValue <= 0) {
                Ui.showError(error, "Длительность должна быть больше нуля");
                return;
            }

            submit.setDisable(true);
            submit.setText("Сохранение…");
            context.async(
                    () -> context.catalog().createService(serviceName, servicePrice, durationValue),
                    service -> {
                        modal.hide();
                        context.toast().success("Услуга «" + service.name() + "» добавлена");
                        load();
                    },
                    throwable -> {
                        submit.setDisable(false);
                        submit.setText("Добавить услугу");
                        Ui.showError(error, AppContext.errorText(throwable));
                    });
        });
    }

    private static boolean contains(String value, String query) {
        return value != null && value.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains(query);
    }
}
``

package org.example.sto.ui.pages;

import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.ComboBox;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.ScrollPane;
import javafx.scene.control.TableView;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.Priority;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.scene.paint.Color;
import javafx.util.StringConverter;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderStatus;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderStatusLogEntry;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderSummary;
import org.example.sto.ui.AppContext;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.Icon;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.TableCells;
import org.example.sto.ui.components.Ui;
import org.example.sto.ui.util.Fmt;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Журнал изменений статусов заявок.
 */
public final class StatusLogView implements PageView {

    private record LogData(
            List<OrderStatusLogEntry> log,
            List<OrderStatus> statuses,
            List<OrderSummary> orders) {
    }

    private final AppContext context;
    private final ScrollPane root;
    private final TableView<OrderStatusLogEntry> table = Ui.table();
    private final ComboBox<OrderSummary> filter = new ComboBox<>();
    private final Label foot = Ui.label("", "table-foot");

    private LogData data = new LogData(List.of(), List.of(), List.of());

    public StatusLogView(AppContext context) {
        this.context = context;

        Button refresh = Ui.ghost("Обновить", Icon.create("refresh", 15));
        refresh.setOnAction(event -> load());

        filter.setConverter(new StringConverter<>() {
            @Override
            public String toString(OrderSummary order) {
                if (order == null) {
                    return "";
                }
                if (order.orderId() == 0) {
                    return "Все заявки";
                }
                return "#" + order.orderId() + " · " + order.client() + " · " + order.car();
            }

            @Override
            public OrderSummary fromString(String string) {
                return null;
            }
        });
        filter.setPrefWidth(320);
        filter.valueProperty().addListener((obs, old, value) -> applyFilter());

        HBox toolbar = new HBox(12,
                Ui.label("Фильтр по заявке", "section-title"),
                filter,
                Ui.spacer(),
                Ui.label("Каждое изменение фиксируется триггером PostgreSQL", "hint"));
        toolbar.getStyleClass().add("card");
        toolbar.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        configureTable();
        VBox tableCard = Ui.card(table, foot);
        VBox.setVgrow(table, Priority.ALWAYS);

        VBox content = Views.page(
                Ui.pageHeader("Аудит", "Журнал статусов",
                        "История изменений, записанная триггерами базы данных", refresh),
                toolbar, tableCard);
        root = Views.scroll(content);
    }

    private void configureTable() {
        table.setPrefHeight(500);
        table.getColumns().add(Ui.column("Время", entry -> Fmt.dateTime(entry.changedAt()), 145));
        table.getColumns().add(Ui.column("Заявка", entry -> "#" + entry.orderId(), 80));
        table.getColumns().add(TableCells.badge("Было", entry -> statusName(entry.oldStatusId()), 115));
        table.getColumns().add(TableCells.badge("Стало", entry -> statusName(entry.newStatusId()), 115));
        table.getColumns().add(Ui.column("Кто изменил", entry -> Fmt.nullSafe(entry.changedBy()), 180));
    }

    @Override
    public Node node() {
        return root;
    }

    @Override
    public void onShow() {
        load();
    }

    private void load() {
        context.async(
                () -> new LogData(
                        context.orders().statusLog(context.user(), null),
                        context.catalog().orderStatuses(),
                        context.orders().listVisible(context.user())),
                loaded -> {
                    data = loaded;
                    List<OrderSummary> items = new ArrayList<>();
                    items.add(new OrderSummary(0, "Все заявки", "", "", BigDecimal.ZERO));
                    items.addAll(loaded.orders());
                    OrderSummary selected = filter.getValue();
                    filter.getItems().setAll(items);
                    if (selected == null || selected.orderId() == 0) {
                        filter.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();
                    } else {
                        items.stream()
                                .filter(order -> order.orderId() == selected.orderId())
                                .findFirst()
                                .ifPresent(filter::setValue);
                    }
                    applyFilter();
                },
                error -> context.toast().error(AppContext.errorText(error)));
    }

    private void applyFilter() {
        OrderSummary selected = filter.getValue();
        int orderId = selected == null ? 0 : selected.orderId();
        List<OrderStatusLogEntry> filtered = data.log().stream()
                .filter(entry -> orderId == 0 || entry.orderId() == orderId)
                .toList();
        table.getItems().setAll(filtered);
        foot.setText("Показано " + filtered.size() + " из " + data.log().size() + " "
                + Fmt.pluralWord(data.log().size(), "события", "событий", "событий"));
        if (filtered.isEmpty()) {
            table.setPlaceholder(Ui.empty("history", "Событий нет",
                    "Журнал заполнится после изменения статуса заявки."));
        }
    }

    private String statusName(Integer statusId) {
        if (statusId == null) {
            return "—";
        }
        return data.statuses().stream()
                .filter(status -> status.id() == statusId)
                .map(OrderStatus::name)
                .findFirst()
                .orElse("—");
    }
}
``

package org.example.sto.ui.pages;

import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.control.ScrollPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;

/**
 * Общие контейнеры страниц.
 */
public final class Views {

    private Views() {
    }

    public static VBox page(Node... children) {
        VBox box = new VBox(16, children);
        box.getStyleClass().addAll("page", "page-content");
        return box;
    }

    public static ScrollPane scroll(Node content) {
        ScrollPane pane = new ScrollPane(content);
        pane.setFitToWidth(true);
        pane.setHbarPolicy(ScrollPane.ScrollBarPolicy.NEVER);
        pane.getStyleClass().add("page-scroll");
        return pane;
    }
}

## 75. `src/main/java/org/example/sto/ui/util/Fmt.java`

Строк: 151

``java
package org.example.sto.ui.util;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.math.RoundingMode;
import java.text.DecimalFormat;
import java.text.DecimalFormatSymbols;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * Единые правила форматирования значений в интерфейсе.
 */
public final class Fmt {

    private static final Locale RU = Locale.of("ru", "RU");
    private static final DateTimeFormatter SHORT_DATE =
            DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("d MMM yyyy", RU);
    private static final DateTimeFormatter DATE_TIME =
            DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("dd.MM.yyyy HH:mm", RU);
    private static final DateTimeFormatter TIME_ONLY =
            DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("HH:mm", RU);
    private static final DecimalFormat MONEY;

    static {
        DecimalFormatSymbols symbols = DecimalFormatSymbols.getInstance(RU);
        symbols.setGroupingSeparator('\u00A0');
        MONEY = new DecimalFormat("#,##0", symbols);
        MONEY.setRoundingMode(RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
    }

    private Fmt() {
    }

    public static String money(BigDecimal value) {
        return money(value, false);
    }

    public static String money(double value) {
        return money(BigDecimal.valueOf(value));
    }

    public static String money(BigDecimal value, boolean withDecimals) {
        if (value == null) {
            value = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        }
        String pattern = withDecimals ? "#,##0.00" : "#,##0";
        DecimalFormat format = new DecimalFormat(pattern, DecimalFormatSymbols.getInstance(RU));
        format.setRoundingMode(RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
        return format.format(value) + " ₽";
    }

    public static String integer(long value) {
        return MONEY.format(value);
    }

    public static String shortDate(LocalDateTime value) {
        return value == null ? "—" : SHORT_DATE.format(value);
    }

    public static String dateTime(LocalDateTime value) {
        return value == null ? "—" : DATE_TIME.format(value);
    }

    public static String time(LocalDateTime value) {
        return value == null ? "—" : TIME_ONLY.format(value);
    }

    public static String pluralWord(long count, String one, String few, String many) {
        long abs = Math.abs(count) % 100;
        long last = abs % 10;
        if (abs > 10 && abs < 20) {
            return many;
        }
        if (last == 1) {
            return one;
        }
        return last >= 2 && last <= 4 ? few : many;
    }

    public static String plural(long count, String one, String few, String many) {
        return count + " " + pluralWord(count, one, few, many);
    }

    public static String initials(String name) {
        if (name == null || name.isBlank()) {
            return "?";
        }
        String[] parts = name.trim().split("\\s+");
        StringBuilder result = new StringBuilder();
        for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(2, parts.length); i++) {
            if (!parts[i].isEmpty()) {
                result.append(Character.toUpperCase(parts[i].charAt(0)));
            }
        }
        return result.isEmpty() ? "?" : result.toString();
    }

    /** Короткое обращение по имени: «Иван Петров» → «Петров». */
    public static String shortName(String fullName) {
        if (fullName == null || fullName.isBlank()) {
            return "коллега";
        }
        String[] parts = fullName.trim().split("\\s+");
        return parts.length > 1 ? parts[1] : parts[0];
    }

    public static String role(String role) {
        if (role == null) {
            return "Пользователь";
        }
        return switch (role) {
            case "admin" -> "Администратор";
            case "manager" -> "Менеджер";
            case "master" -> "Мастер";
            case "client" -> "Клиент";
            default -> "Пользователь";
        };
    }

    public static String nullSafe(String value) {
        return value == null || value.isBlank() ? "—" : value;
    }

    public static String statusClass(String status) {
        if (status == null) {
            return "neutral";
        }
        return switch (status) {
            case "новая" -> "new";
            case "в работе" -> "progress";
            case "завершена" -> "done";
            case "отменена" -> "cancel";
            default -> "neutral";
        };
    }

    public static BigDecimal parseMoney(String value) {
        String normalized = value == null ? "" : value.replace('\u00A0', ' ')
                .replace(" ", "")
                .replace(',', '.')
                .replace("₽", "")
                .trim();
        return normalized.isEmpty() ? null : new BigDecimal(normalized);
    }

    public static Integer parseInteger(String value) {
        String normalized = value == null ? "" : value.trim();
        return normalized.isEmpty() ? null : Integer.valueOf(normalized);
    }
}
``

package org.example.sto;

import org.example.sto.config.AppConfig;
import org.example.sto.config.DbSettings;
import org.example.sto.db.ConnectionManager;
import org.example.sto.db.RetryPolicy;
import org.example.sto.exception.AccessDeniedException;
import org.example.sto.exception.AuthenticationException;
import org.example.sto.exception.DataIntegrityException;
import org.example.sto.exception.ForeignKeyViolationException;
import org.example.sto.exception.ValidationException;
import org.example.sto.model.Car;
import org.example.sto.model.Client;
import org.example.sto.model.ClientOrder;
import org.example.sto.model.Order;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderSummary;
import org.example.sto.model.Service;
import org.example.sto.model.User;
import org.example.sto.repository.CarRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.ClientRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.MasterRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.OrderRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.ServiceRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.UserRepository;
import org.example.sto.service.AuthService;
import org.example.sto.service.CatalogService;
import org.example.sto.service.OrderService;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterAll;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assumptions;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeAll;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.TestInstance;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/**
 * Интеграционные тесты связки «Java-приложение — PostgreSQL».
 *
 * <p>Перед запуском должен быть поднят стенд:
 * {@code docker compose up -d}. Если БД недоступна, тесты помечаются
 * как пропущенные (Assumption), а не падают.
 */
@TestInstance(TestInstance.Lifecycle.PER_CLASS)
class DatabaseIntegrationTest {

    private static ConnectionManager connectionManager;
    private static UserRepository userRepository;
    private static ClientRepository clientRepository;
    private static CarRepository carRepository;
    private static ServiceRepository serviceRepository;
    private static OrderRepository orderRepository;
    private static AuthService authService;
    private static CatalogService catalogService;
    private static OrderService orderService;

    @BeforeAll
    void connect() {
        DbSettings settings = AppConfig.load().dbSettings();
        ConnectionManager manager = new ConnectionManager(
                settings, new RetryPolicy(1, 0, 1.0, 0));
        try {
            manager.ping();
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            manager.close();
            Assumptions.assumeTrue(false,
                    "PostgreSQL недоступен (" + settings.url() + "). Запустите docker compose up -d. " + e.getMessage());
            return;
        }

        connectionManager = manager;
        userRepository = new UserRepository(manager);
        clientRepository = new ClientRepository(manager);
        carRepository = new CarRepository(manager);
        MasterRepository masterRepository = new MasterRepository(manager);
        serviceRepository = new ServiceRepository(manager);
        orderRepository = new OrderRepository(manager);

        authService = new AuthService(userRepository);
        catalogService = new CatalogService(
                clientRepository, carRepository, serviceRepository, masterRepository, orderRepository);
        orderService = new OrderService(
                orderRepository, clientRepository, carRepository, masterRepository, serviceRepository);
    }

    @AfterAll
    void disconnect() {
        if (connectionManager != null) {
            connectionManager.close();
        }
    }

    @Test
    void authenticatesSeededAdmin() {
        User user = authService.login("admin", "admin123".toCharArray());

        assertEquals("admin", user.username());
        assertEquals("admin", user.role());
        assertTrue(user.fullName().contains("Поляков"));

        // Логин не зависит от регистра: удобно при вводе с клавиатуры.
        User upperCaseLogin = authService.login("Admin", "admin123".toCharArray());
        assertEquals("admin", upperCaseLogin.username());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsWrongPassword() {
        assertThrows(AuthenticationException.class,
                () -> authService.login("admin", "wrong-password".toCharArray()));
    }

    @Test
    void clientAccountIsBoundToClientCard() {
        User user = authService.login("client", "client123".toCharArray());

        assertEquals("client", user.role());
        assertNotNull(user.clientId());
        assertEquals("Иванов Иван Иванович", catalogService.clientById(user.clientId()).fullName());
    }

    @Test
    void clientSeesOnlyOwnOrders() {
        User user = authService.login("client", "client123".toCharArray());
        List<OrderSummary> visible = orderService.listVisible(user);
        List<Integer> ownOrderIds = orderService.clientOrders(user.clientId()).stream()
                .map(ClientOrder::orderId)
                .toList();

        assertFalse(visible.isEmpty());
        assertTrue(visible.stream().allMatch(order -> ownOrderIds.contains(order.orderId())));
    }

    @Test
    void clientCannotOpenForeignOrder() {
        User user = authService.login("client", "client123".toCharArray());
        Order foreign = orderService.listAll().stream()
                .map(summary -> orderService.getOrder(summary.orderId()))
                .filter(order -> order.clientId() != user.clientId())
                .findFirst()
                .orElseThrow();

        assertThrows(AccessDeniedException.class,
                () -> orderService.getOrderVisible(user, foreign.id()));
    }

    @Test
    void sqlInjectionInLoginDoesNotBypassAuthentication() {
        assertThrows(AuthenticationException.class,
                () -> authService.login("' OR '1'='1", "anything".toCharArray()));
        assertThrows(AuthenticationException.class,
                () -> authService.login("admin' --", "anything".toCharArray()));
        assertThrows(AuthenticationException.class,
                () -> authService.login("admin", "' OR '1'='1".toCharArray()));
    }

    @Test
    void readsOrdersFromView() {
        List<OrderSummary> orders = orderService.listAll();

        assertFalse(orders.isEmpty());
        assertTrue(orders.stream().anyMatch(order -> order.status().equals("в работе")));
        assertTrue(orders.stream().allMatch(order -> order.totalCost() != null));
    }

    @Test
    void viewTotalEqualsStoredFunctionResult() {
        OrderSummary summary = orderService.listAll().stream()
                .filter(order -> order.totalCost().signum() > 0)
                .findFirst()
                .orElseThrow();

        assertEquals(0, summary.totalCost().compareTo(orderService.calculateTotal(summary.orderId())));
    }

    @Test
    void returnsClientOrdersThroughStoredFunction() {
        Client client = catalogService.findClientByPhone("+7-900-100-10-01");
        List<ClientOrder> orders = orderService.clientOrders(client.id());

        assertFalse(orders.isEmpty());
        assertTrue(orders.stream().allMatch(order -> order.carPlate() != null && !order.carPlate().isBlank()));
    }

    @Test
    void createsAddsServiceAndClosesOrder() throws SQLException {
        Client client = catalogService.findClientByPhone("+7-900-100-10-01");
        Car car = catalogService.clientCars(client.id()).get(0);
        Service service = catalogService.services().get(0);

        int orderId = orderService.createOrder(
                client.id(), car.id(), null, "Интеграционный тест: создание и закрытие заявки");
        try {
            orderService.addService(orderId, service.id(), 2);

            BigDecimal expected = service.price().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(2));
            assertEquals(0, expected.compareTo(orderService.calculateTotal(orderId)));

            orderService.closeOrder(orderId);
            Order closed = orderService.getOrder(orderId);
            assertEquals("завершена", closed.statusName());
            assertNotNull(closed.closedAt());

            assertTrue(orderService.statusLog(orderId).stream()
                    .anyMatch(entry -> entry.newStatusId() == 3));
        } finally {
            cleanupOrder(orderId);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void preventsAddingServiceToClosedOrder() {
        Order closedOrder = orderService.listAll().stream()
                .filter(order -> order.status().equals("завершена"))
                .findFirst()
                .map(summary -> orderService.getOrder(summary.orderId()))
                .orElseThrow();

        Service service = catalogService.services().get(0);
        assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                () -> orderService.addService(closedOrder.id(), service.id(), 1));
    }

    @Test
    void createsClientAndCar() {
        String phone = "+7-999-000-00-88";
        deleteClientByPhone(phone);
        Client client = catalogService.createClient("Тестовый Владелец", phone, "owner@example.ru");
        try {
            Car car = catalogService.createCar(
                    client.id(), "Test", "Model", 2020, "Т888ТТ88", "TESTVIN1234567890");
            assertEquals(client.id(), car.clientId());
            assertEquals("Т888ТТ88", car.plateNum());
            assertFalse(catalogService.clientCars(client.id()).isEmpty());
        } finally {
            clientRepository.delete(client.id());
        }
    }

    @Test
    void createsOrderWithoutMaster() throws SQLException {
        Client client = catalogService.findClientByPhone("+7-900-100-10-01");
        Car car = catalogService.clientCars(client.id()).get(0);

        int orderId = orderService.createOrder(
                client.id(), car.id(), null, "Заявка без назначенного мастера");
        try {
            Order order = orderService.getOrder(orderId);
            assertEquals("новая", order.statusName());
            assertEquals(null, order.masterId());
        } finally {
            cleanupOrder(orderId);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void transactionRollsBackOnRuntimeException() {
        String phone = "+7-999-000-00-99";
        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, () ->
                connectionManager.inTransaction("test.rollback", connection -> {
                    try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(
                            "INSERT INTO clients (full_name, phone) VALUES (?, ?)")) {
                        statement.setString(1, "Откат");
                        statement.setString(2, phone);
                        statement.executeUpdate();
                    }
                    throw new IllegalStateException("Искусственная ошибка после INSERT");
                }));

        assertTrue(clientRepository.findByPhone(phone).isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void databaseRejectsTooLongVarchar() {
        DataIntegrityException exception = assertThrows(DataIntegrityException.class, () ->
                connectionManager.withConnection("test.varchar", connection -> {
                    try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(
                            "INSERT INTO clients (full_name, phone) VALUES ('Длина', ?)")) {
                        statement.setString(1, "1".repeat(21));
                        return statement.executeUpdate();
                    }
                }));

        assertEquals("22001", exception.getSqlState());
    }

    @Test
    void databaseRejectsInvalidYearCheckConstraint() {
        DataIntegrityException exception = assertThrows(DataIntegrityException.class, () ->
                carRepository.create(1, "Test", "Year", 1800, "У777УУ77", null));

        assertEquals("23514", exception.getSqlState());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsDuplicateClientPhone() {
        String phone = "+7-999-000-00-77";
        deleteClientByPhone(phone);
        Client first = catalogService.createClient("Тестовый Клиент", phone, "test@example.ru");
        try {
            assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                    () -> catalogService.createClient("Другой Клиент", phone, "other@example.ru"));
        } finally {
            clientRepository.delete(first.id());
        }
    }

    @Test
    void databaseRejectsForeignKeyViolation() {
        assertThrows(ForeignKeyViolationException.class,
                () -> carRepository.create(999_999, "Test", "FK", 2020, "Т999ТТ99", null));
    }

    @Test
    void databaseRejectsNullInNotNullColumn() {
        DataIntegrityException exception = assertThrows(DataIntegrityException.class,
                () -> connectionManager.withConnection("test.notNull", connection -> {
                    try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(
                            "INSERT INTO clients (full_name, phone) VALUES ('Без телефона', NULL)")) {
                        return statement.executeUpdate();
                    }
                }));

        assertEquals("23502", exception.getSqlState());
    }

    private void cleanupOrder(int orderId) throws SQLException {
        connectionManager.inTransaction("test.cleanupOrder", connection -> {
            try (PreparedStatement services = connection.prepareStatement(
                    "DELETE FROM order_services WHERE order_id = ?");
                 PreparedStatement log = connection.prepareStatement(
                         "DELETE FROM order_status_log WHERE order_id = ?");
                 PreparedStatement order = connection.prepareStatement(
                         "DELETE FROM orders WHERE id = ?")) {
                services.setInt(1, orderId);
                services.executeUpdate();
                log.setInt(1, orderId);
                log.executeUpdate();
                order.setInt(1, orderId);
                order.executeUpdate();
            }
            return null;
        });
    }

    private void deleteClientByPhone(String phone) {
        connectionManager.withConnection("test.cleanupClient", connection -> {
            try (PreparedStatement services = connection.prepareStatement(
                    "DELETE FROM order_services WHERE order_id IN (SELECT id FROM orders WHERE client_id IN "
                            + "(SELECT id FROM clients WHERE phone = ?))");
                 PreparedStatement log = connection.prepareStatement(
                         "DELETE FROM order_status_log WHERE order_id IN (SELECT id FROM orders WHERE client_id IN "
                                 + "(SELECT id FROM clients WHERE phone = ?))");
                 PreparedStatement orders = connection.prepareStatement(
                         "DELETE FROM orders WHERE client_id IN (SELECT id FROM clients WHERE phone = ?)");
                 PreparedStatement cars = connection.prepareStatement(
                         "DELETE FROM cars WHERE client_id IN (SELECT id FROM clients WHERE phone = ?)");
                 PreparedStatement clients = connection.prepareStatement(
                         "DELETE FROM clients WHERE phone = ?")) {
                for (PreparedStatement statement : List.of(services, log, orders, cars, clients)) {
                    statement.setString(1, phone);
                    statement.executeUpdate();
                }
            }
            return null;
        });
    }
}
``

``java
package org.example.sto.config;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

class DbSettingsTest {

    @Test
    void readsClasspathConfiguration() {
        DbSettings settings = AppConfig.load().dbSettings();

        assertTrue(settings.url().startsWith("jdbc:postgresql://"));
        assertEquals("sto_admin", settings.username());
        assertEquals(5, settings.maximumPoolSize());
        assertEquals(4, settings.retryMaxAttempts());
    }

    @Test
    void doesNotExposePasswordInSafeString() {
        DbSettings settings = new DbSettings(
                "jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5434/sto", "sto_admin", "secret",
                "org.postgresql.Driver", 5, 1, 3000, 1000, 60000, 600000,
                4, 250, 2.0, 4000);

        assertTrue(settings.toSafeString().contains("sto_admin"));
        assertTrue(!settings.toSafeString().contains("secret"));
    }

    @Test
    void validatesMandatoryParameters() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new DbSettings(
                "", "user", "pass", "org.postgresql.Driver", 5, 1, 3000, 1000,
                60000, 600000, 4, 250, 2.0, 4000));

        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new DbSettings(
                "jdbc:postgresql://localhost/sto", "user", "pass", "org.postgresql.Driver",
                0, 0, 3000, 1000, 60000, 600000, 4, 250, 2.0, 4000));
    }
}
``

## 78. `src/test/java/org/example/sto/db/RetryPolicyTest.java`

Строк: 61

``java
package org.example.sto.db;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.net.ConnectException;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.sql.SQLTransientConnectionException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

class RetryPolicyTest {

    @Test
    void delayGrowsExponentiallyAndIsCapped() {
        RetryPolicy policy = new RetryPolicy(6, 250, 2.0, 1_000);

        assertEquals(250, policy.delayMillis(1));
        assertEquals(500, policy.delayMillis(2));
        assertEquals(1_000, policy.delayMillis(3));
        assertEquals(1_000, policy.delayMillis(4));
    }

    @Test
    void sleeperReceivesCalculatedDelay() throws InterruptedException {
        List<Long> delays = new ArrayList<>();
        RetryPolicy policy = new RetryPolicy(4, 100, 2.0, 10_000, delays::add);

        policy.sleepBeforeRetry(1);
        policy.sleepBeforeRetry(2);

        assertEquals(List.of(100L, 200L), delays);
    }

    @Test
    void retriesConnectionLoss() {
        assertTrue(RetryPolicy.isRetryable(new SQLException("connection lost", "08006")));
        assertTrue(RetryPolicy.isRetryable(new SQLTransientConnectionException("pool exhausted")));
        assertTrue(RetryPolicy.isRetryable(new ConnectException("refused")));
        assertTrue(RetryPolicy.isRetryable(new SQLException("deadlock", "40P01")));
        assertTrue(RetryPolicy.isRetryable(new RuntimeException(new SQLException("serialization", "40001"))));
    }

    @Test
    void doesNotRetryPermanentErrors() {
        assertFalse(RetryPolicy.isRetryable(new SQLException("duplicate", "23505")));
        assertFalse(RetryPolicy.isRetryable(new SQLException("syntax", "42601")));
        assertFalse(RetryPolicy.isRetryable(new IllegalArgumentException("bad input")));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsInvalidConfiguration() {
        org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -> new RetryPolicy(0, 100, 2.0, 1_000));
        org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -> new RetryPolicy(3, 100, 0.5, 1_000));
    }
}
``

## 79. `src/test/java/org/example/sto/db/SqlExceptionTranslatorTest.java`

Строк: 84

``java
package org.example.sto.db;

import org.example.sto.exception.ConnectionUnavailableException;
import org.example.sto.exception.DataIntegrityException;
import org.example.sto.exception.DatabaseException;
import org.example.sto.exception.DuplicateKeyException;
import org.example.sto.exception.ForeignKeyViolationException;
import org.example.sto.exception.QueryException;
import org.example.sto.exception.TransientDatabaseException;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.sql.SQLException;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertInstanceOf;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

class SqlExceptionTranslatorTest {

    @Test
    void translatesUniqueViolation() {
        DatabaseException result = SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(
                new SQLException("duplicate key value violates unique constraint \"clients_phone_key\"", "23505"),
                "clients.create");

        assertInstanceOf(DuplicateKeyException.class, result);
        assertEquals("23505", result.getSqlState());
        assertEquals("clients.create", result.getOperation());
    }

    @Test
    void translatesForeignKeyViolation() {
        DatabaseException result = SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(
                new SQLException("insert or update violates foreign key", "23503"), "cars.create");
        assertInstanceOf(ForeignKeyViolationException.class, result);
    }

    @Test
    void translatesNotNullAndLengthViolationsToIntegrityError() {
        assertInstanceOf(DataIntegrityException.class,
                SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(new SQLException("null value", "23502"), "test"));
        assertInstanceOf(DataIntegrityException.class,
                SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(new SQLException("value too long", "22001"), "test"));
    }

    @Test
    void translatesConnectionProblems() {
        assertInstanceOf(ConnectionUnavailableException.class,
                SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(new SQLException("connection refused", "08001"), "connect"));
        assertInstanceOf(ConnectionUnavailableException.class,
                SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(new SQLException("starting up", "57P03"), "connect"));
    }

    @Test
    void translatesSyntaxErrors() {
        DatabaseException result = SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(
                new SQLException("syntax error at or near \"SELEC\"", "42601"), "query");
        assertInstanceOf(QueryException.class, result);
        assertTrue(result.getMessage().contains("SELEC"));
    }

    @Test
    void translatesSerializationFailure() {
        assertInstanceOf(TransientDatabaseException.class,
                SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(new SQLException("could not serialize", "40001"), "update"));
    }

    @Test
    void fallsBackToGenericDatabaseException() {
        DatabaseException result = SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(
                new SQLException("unknown", "XX000"), "unknown.operation");
        assertEquals(DatabaseException.class, result.getClass());
        assertEquals("XX000", result.getSqlState());
    }

    @Test
    void usesSqlStateFromNextExceptionChain() {
        SQLException wrapper = new SQLException("batch failed", "99999");
        wrapper.setNextException(new SQLException("duplicate", "23505"));

        DatabaseException result = SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(wrapper, "batch");
        assertInstanceOf(DuplicateKeyException.class, result);
    }
}
``

## 80. `src/test/java/org/example/sto/model/RolePermissionsTest.java`

Строк: 70

``java
package org.example.sto.model;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

class RolePermissionsTest {

    @Test
    void adminCanDoEverything() {
        RolePermissions admin = RolePermissions.of("admin");

        for (Permission permission : Permission.values()) {
            assertTrue(admin.can(permission), "admin должен иметь право " + permission);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void managerManagesOrdersAndCatalogsButNotPriceList() {
        RolePermissions manager = RolePermissions.of("manager");

        assertTrue(manager.can(Permission.CREATE_ORDER));
        assertTrue(manager.can(Permission.CREATE_CLIENT));
        assertTrue(manager.can(Permission.CREATE_CAR));
        assertTrue(manager.can(Permission.VIEW_LOG));
        assertFalse(manager.can(Permission.CREATE_SERVICE));
    }

    @Test
    void masterWorksWithOrdersButDoesNotManageCatalogs() {
        RolePermissions master = RolePermissions.of("master");

        assertTrue(master.can(Permission.VIEW_ORDERS));
        assertTrue(master.can(Permission.ADD_ORDER_SERVICE));
        assertTrue(master.can(Permission.CLOSE_ORDER));
        assertFalse(master.can(Permission.CREATE_ORDER));
        assertFalse(master.can(Permission.CREATE_CLIENT));
        assertFalse(master.can(Permission.CREATE_CAR));
        assertFalse(master.can(Permission.CREATE_SERVICE));
        assertFalse(master.can(Permission.VIEW_LOG));
    }

    @Test
    void clientSeesOnlyOwnOrdersAndPriceList() {
        RolePermissions client = RolePermissions.of("client");

        assertTrue(client.can(Permission.VIEW_ORDERS));
        assertTrue(client.can(Permission.VIEW_SERVICES));
        assertFalse(client.can(Permission.VIEW_ALL_ORDERS));
        assertFalse(client.can(Permission.VIEW_DASHBOARD));
        assertFalse(client.can(Permission.VIEW_CLIENTS));
        assertFalse(client.can(Permission.VIEW_CARS));
        assertFalse(client.can(Permission.VIEW_MASTERS));
        assertFalse(client.can(Permission.VIEW_LOG));
        assertFalse(client.can(Permission.CREATE_ORDER));
        assertFalse(client.can(Permission.ADD_ORDER_SERVICE));
        assertFalse(client.can(Permission.CLOSE_ORDER));
    }

    @Test
    void unknownRoleHasNoAccess() {
        RolePermissions guest = RolePermissions.of("unknown");

        assertFalse(guest.hasAnySection());
        for (Permission permission : Permission.values()) {
            assertFalse(guest.can(permission));
        }
    }
}
``

``java
package org.example.sto.service;

import org.example.sto.exception.ValidationException;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

/**
 * Тесты бизнес-валидации, не требующие подключения к БД:
 * проверки выполняются до обращения к репозиториям.
 */
class ValidationTest {

    private final CatalogService catalogService =
            new CatalogService(null, null, null, null, null);
    private final OrderService orderService =
            new OrderService(null, null, null, null, null);

    @Test
    void rejectsEmptyClientName() {
        assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                () -> catalogService.createClient("  ", "+7-900-000-00-00", null));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsInvalidPhone() {
        assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                () -> catalogService.createClient("Иванов Иван", "телефон", null));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNullServicePrice() {
        assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                () -> catalogService.createService("Услуга", null, 30));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNegativeServicePrice() {
        assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                () -> catalogService.createService("Услуга", new BigDecimal("-1.00"), 30));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNonPositiveQuantity() {
        assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                () -> orderService.addService(1, 1, 0));
        assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                () -> orderService.addService(1, 1, -5));
        assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                () -> orderService.addService(1, 1, 101));
    }
}
``
